To sponsor the proposed budget of the Office of the President, the Senior Vice Chair of the Committee on Appropriations, the Hon. Abet Garcia of the 2nd District of Bataan, is hereby recognized.

**[00:23:14.280] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000085)  
Thank you very much, Madam Speaker, Majority Floor Leader, Distinguished Guests and Colleagues. Magandang umaga po sa ating lahat.

**[00:23:22.940] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000086)  
On behalf of the Committee on Appropriations, I have the honor to sponsor the proposed fiscal year 2027 budget of the Office of the President.

**[00:23:34.400] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000087)  
The Office of the President is the apex of the executive branch, the office from which the President as Chief Executive and Commander-in-Chief directs the entire government.

**[00:23:50.940] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000088)  
What we appropriate for it?

**[00:23:52.940] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000089)  
We appropriate for the working of the presidency itself.

**[00:23:58.420] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000090)  
The 2027 budget, the office proposes a 10,155,758,000 or 10.16 billion inclusive of the retirement and life insurance premium.

**[00:24:22.940] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000092)  
That is about 0.14% of the 7.2 trillion national budget, roughly one-seventh of 1%.

**[00:24:36.600] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000093)  
For the highest office of the land, this is a lean proposal, Madam Speaker.

**[00:24:45.440] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000094)  
Its shape is simple.

**[00:24:46.740] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000095)  
By allotment class, it is an operating budget.

**[00:24:51.620] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000096)  
Maintenance and other operations.

**[00:24:52.940] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000097)  
Operating expense account for 7.46 billion or 74%.

**[00:24:59.240] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000098)  
Personal services comprise of 1.86 billion or 18%.

**[00:25:05.320] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000099)  
Capital outlay stands at 839.66 million or a mere 8%.

**[00:25:13.940] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000100)  
By source, it is almost entirely new appropriations at 99.29%.

**[00:25:21.220] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000101)  
Placed squarely.

**[00:25:22.940] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000102)  
Before this chamber for scrutiny.

**[00:25:28.100] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000103)  
What does it fund?

**[00:25:30.600] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000104)  
Two pillars, Madam Speaker.

**[00:25:32.640] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000105)  
General administration and support at 2.74 billion and operations at 7.41 billion delivered through four programs.

**[00:25:43.520] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000106)  
The largest is the Presidential Oversight Program at 5.37 billion.

**[00:25:49.560] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000107)  
It covers directives and clearances.

**[00:25:52.940] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000108)  
Policy on national interventions.

**[00:25:56.640] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000109)  
Oversight of national security.

**[00:25:59.700] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000110)  
Public assistance and performance monitoring across the executive.

**[00:26:05.680] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000111)  
Next is the Executive Staff Service at 1.63 billion.

**[00:26:10.460] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000112)  
Covering the President's missions and state visits.

**[00:26:14.880] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000113)  
Security, official events and documents.

**[00:26:19.320] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000114)  
The Advisory Program at 184.6 million

**[00:26:22.940] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000115)  
and the Legal and Legislative Services Program at 180 million.

**[00:26:29.940] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000116)  
Complete operations.

**[00:26:31.940] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000117)  
The latter funds the review of the very bills we pass.

**[00:26:37.940] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000118)  
And the office binds itself to account for every peso.

**[00:26:41.940] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000119)  
Under its special provision, it must file quarterly reports on its financial and physical accomplishments within 30 days of each quarter.

**[00:26:52.940] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000120)  
Post them online and notify both houses and their committees on appropriations and finance.

**[00:27:01.940] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000121)  
Transparency here is not a promise, it is a condition of the appropriation.

**[00:27:11.940] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000122)  
Now to what my colleagues will notice at once and what I present with pride, not apology, on a comparable basis.

**[00:27:22.240] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000123)  
Net of retirement

**[00:27:24.280] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000124)  
and life insurance premium

**[00:27:26.200] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000125)  
and pension,

**[00:27:27.880] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000128)  
The office's budget falls from 28.03 billion to 10.08 billion.

**[00:27:33.800] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000129)  
And that is a reduction of 17.95 billion, or 64.02%.

**[00:27:41.440] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000130)  
The Office of the President is asking for

**[00:27:46.440] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000131)  
marked less.

**[00:27:49.440] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000132)  
And that's a reason.

**[00:27:50.440] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000133)  
The reason is plain. Some P17.55 billion of that drop is the completion of the locally funded projects in 2026 budget, principally our hosting of the ASEAN 2026.

**[00:28:08.240] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000134)  
That duty is successfully done. Not a single peso of it is carried in the 2027 budget. And even setting ASEAN aside, the regular programs were trimmed by a further 392.52 million or 3.75%.

**[00:28:29.540] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000135)  
This is an office that tightened its own belt before asking our people or any agency.

**[00:28:38.240] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000136)  
Madam Speaker, this is a budget that funds the presidency at a fraction of the national total, carried an unchanged workforce, bound to quarterly transparency, and smaller than the year before.

**[00:28:56.700] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000137)  
I stand ready, together with the resource person headed by our Executive Secretary, Ralph Recto, to answer any questions in this chamber.

**[00:29:08.020] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000138)  
We thank you.

**[00:29:08.240] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000139)  
With that, I respectfully sponsor and commend the fiscal year 2027 budget of the Office of the President and move for its favorable consideration.

**[00:29:19.620] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000140)  
Madam Speaker, maraming salamat at magandang umaga po sa ating lahat.

**[00:29:24.100] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000141)  
Majority Leader.

**[00:29:25.480] Rep. Ryan S. Recto** (asr-000142)  
Madam Speaker, I move to recognize the Honorable Rene Co. of the Kabataan Party List to interpolate the sponsor.

**[00:29:31.260] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-000143)

**[00:29:33.380] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000144)  
Thank you, Madam Speaker. At magandang umaga po.

**[00:29:38.240] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000145)  
The distinguished sponsor natin.

**[00:29:40.060] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000146)  
Will the distinguished sponsor yield to a few questions in relation to the budget of the Office of the President?

**[00:29:44.900] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000147)  
Yes, Madam Speaker. At magandang umaga po.

**[00:29:48.620] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000148)  
Madam Speaker, ang ating mga katanungan dito ay mga natanong na rin natin over the course of the budget cycle.

**[00:29:55.740] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000149)  
Ngunit sa mga paglalatag ng mga...

**[00:30:00.000] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000150)  
mandato, the Office of the President would be in the best position to answer these questions.

**[00:30:07.240] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000151)  
Ang una nating tanong ay patungkol po sa Barangay Development Program to support the

**[00:30:12.780] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000152)  
activities of the National Task Force to End Local Communist Armed Conflict or NTF-ELCAC.

**[00:30:18.100] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000153)  
Bilang chairman ng NTF-ELCAC ang Pangulo.

**[00:30:22.240] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000154)  
Alam ba ng Pangulo kung ano ang proseso ng pagpapaklear ng mga barangay para maging

**[00:30:28.020] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000155)  
eligible beneficiaries ng Barangay Development Program?

**[00:30:33.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000156)  
Yes, Madam Speaker.

**[00:30:34.980] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000157)  
Maari po bang madetali, Madam Speaker, Distinguished Sponsor, yung proseso na ito?

**[00:30:40.860] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000158)  
Well, one of the major process, Madam Speaker, ito po'y dumadaan sa mga local peace and

**[00:30:48.360] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000159)  
development councils from the LGU up to the region at umakit po ito sa National Peace

**[00:30:55.780] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000160)  
and Order Council.

**[00:30:58.020] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000161)  
And ito po ang nagde-declare ng insurgency-free sa ating mga localities.

**[00:31:06.100] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000162)  
And kapag ito po'y nadeclare na insurgency-free, they would be eligible for the fund under

**[00:31:13.660] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000163)  
the LGSF or yung Barangay Development Program, Madam Speaker.

**[00:31:19.220] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000164)  
Madam Speaker, good sponsor, tinanong po kasi natin sa DILG.

**[00:31:22.860] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000165)  
Bilang sila po yung nagkukulit ng listahan.

**[00:31:27.300] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000166)  
Tinanong rin po.

**[00:31:28.020] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000167)  
At tinanong natin sa DBM.

**[00:31:29.460] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000168)  
Bilang sila po yung tumatanggap ng listahan at later sila ang magbibigay ng pondo or

**[00:31:36.260] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000169)  
mag-aalat ng pondo sa NEP para mabigay ng grant yung nasa loob ng program.

**[00:31:44.560] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000170)  
Ang sabi po nila, Barangays undergo community support programs and retooled community support

**[00:31:51.520] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000171)  
programs led by DILG.

**[00:31:53.960] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000172)  
At ang listahan, pagka natapos na...

**[00:31:58.020] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000173)  
Pagka natapos na yung sumisyon na ito, ay i-e-endorso ng NTF-LCAC to DBM.

**[00:32:04.520] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000174)  
So ang tanong natin, Madam Speaker, good sponsor, ito lang ba ang requirement para maging eligible?

**[00:32:10.160] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000175)  
Automatically, in-e-endorse lang din ba ng NTF-LCAC kung ano mang mga listahan yung makarating sa kanila?

**[00:32:18.640] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000176)  
Ano pong mga iba't-ibang proseso para ma-verify na kung ano man po yung nakalagay sa listahan,

**[00:32:25.940] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000177)  
kung ano pong activity yung nangyayari?

**[00:32:28.020] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000178)  
Sa mga local peace councils ay naisagawa po. Madam Speaker, good sponsor.

**[00:32:37.860] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000179)  
Sa mga local development... Sorry.

**[00:32:41.040] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000180)  
Sa mga local peace and order council, Madam Speaker, distinguished colleagues,

**[00:32:46.100] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000181)  
kasama po dito ang armed forces, PNP, and other agencies,

**[00:32:51.260] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000182)  
and they validate in context itong mga insurgency clearance.

**[00:32:58.020] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000183)  
They're LGUs.

**[00:33:00.800] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000184)  
Bukod po dyan, yung ating mga peace and order councils is chaired by the mayors, the governors,

**[00:33:08.620] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000185)  
kaya ito po'y may konteksto.

**[00:33:11.140] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000186)  
And once, together with the armed forces, these localities are validated as insurgency-free,

**[00:33:20.420] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000187)  
they will be eligible for the Barangay Development Program assistance, Madam Speaker.

**[00:33:28.020] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000188)  
So, from the ground up po ito, at kapag na-validate po ito from the ground up,

**[00:33:34.280] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000189)  
dun pa lang po umaakyat sa ating NDFL-CAC, and they refer this to DBM and DILG

**[00:33:42.980] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000190)  
para po maging eligible dito po sa Barangay Development Program Fund.

**[00:33:48.200] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000191)  
Madam Speaker, good sponsor. Ano pong ibig sabihin ng in context, validated in context?

**[00:33:52.860] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000192)  
Maari po bang makapaglatag ng isang senaryo na nasabi ng AFD?

**[00:33:58.020] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000193)  
PNP, PNP, other agencies do that kind of validation habang, ano po, pag ginagawa po yung activity?

**[00:34:05.900] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000194)  
Madam Speaker, good sponsor.

**[00:34:07.620] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000195)  
Yeah. What I mean, Madam Speaker, na in context, kasi it's very hard for the national government

**[00:34:13.520] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000196)  
or the national level to validate yung pagiging insurgency-free ng isang lugar

**[00:34:20.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000197)  
from the macro level or the national level.

**[00:34:23.400] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000198)  
So, the people on the ground know the situation best.

**[00:34:28.020] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000199)  
And these are the local government officials, the mayors, the barangay officials,

**[00:34:34.040] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000200)

**[00:34:39.380] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000201)

**[00:34:44.940] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000202)

**[00:34:49.600] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000203)

**[00:35:00.360] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000207)

**[00:35:05.340] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000208)

**[00:35:11.960] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000209)

**[00:35:19.120] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000210)

**[00:35:26.240] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000211)

**[00:35:28.000] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000212)

**[00:35:31.320] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000213)

**[00:35:39.840] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000214)  
Madam Speaker, good sponsor.

**[00:35:41.720] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000215)

**[00:35:45.280] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000216)

**[00:35:50.040] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000218)

**[00:35:58.000] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000219)

**[00:35:59.400] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000220)

**[00:36:04.800] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000221)

**[00:36:14.100] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000222)

**[00:36:21.800] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000223)

**[00:36:28.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000224)  
Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleagues.

**[00:36:51.460] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000225)

**[00:36:54.920] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000226)

**[00:36:58.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000227)

**[00:37:15.300] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000228)

**[00:37:23.440] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000229)

**[00:37:31.040] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000230)

**[00:37:35.040] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000231)  
Peace and Order and Public Safety Plan, a three-year term-based plan,

**[00:37:40.740] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000232)

**[00:37:49.440] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000233)

**[00:37:53.280] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000234)

**[00:38:53.280] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000244)

**[00:39:03.900] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000245)  
Pero Madam Speaker, good sponsor, ang tanong po natin ay yung three-year term-based plan.

**[00:39:07.520] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000246)  
Yes, Madam Speaker, distinguished colleagues.

**[00:39:10.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000247)  
Yung three-year term-based plan, ang nagpo-formulate po nito ay yung Barangay Development Council.

**[00:39:17.340] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000248)

**[00:39:21.580] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000249)

**[00:39:36.380] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000252)

**[00:39:39.320] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000253)

**[00:39:45.420] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000254)

**[00:39:52.580] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000255)

**[00:39:57.320] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000256)

**[00:40:04.700] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-000257)

**[00:55:07.700] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000592)

**[00:55:10.580] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000593)  
dahil sa laki ng bigat

**[00:55:12.540] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000594)  
ng mataas na presyo

**[00:55:15.080] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000595)  
ng prudo sa mga magsasaka

**[00:55:17.020] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000596)  
at mga manginisda sa kanilang

**[00:55:18.720] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000597)  
pagtatanim at sa kanilang paglaot.

**[00:55:21.080] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000598)  
Ang diesel at gasolin din, Madam Speaker,

**[00:55:23.540] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000599)  
ang itinuturing na

**[00:55:24.860] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000600)

**[00:55:26.160] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000601)  
ng halagah ng transportasyon

**[00:55:28.280] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000602)  
at dahil dito ang presyo ng pagkain

**[00:55:30.940] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000603)  
at mga pangunahin bilihin,

**[00:55:32.920] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000604)  
maari po bang magbigay

**[00:55:34.880] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000605)  
ang Office of the President

**[00:55:36.460] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000606)  
ng espesipiko

**[00:55:38.280] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000607)  
na pecha

**[00:55:40.200] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000608)  
kung kailan po natin maasahan

**[00:55:42.200] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000609)  
na dapat naman sana, ginawa na rin

**[00:55:44.660] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000610)  
ng Marso

**[00:55:45.880] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000611)  
ng taon na ito at ngayon,

**[00:55:48.240] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000612)  
lalong-lalo na pumapalo na naman

**[00:55:49.960] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000613)  
nasa isang daan

**[00:55:52.000] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000614)  
kada litro ang diesel,

**[00:55:54.860] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000615)  
na napakabigat po nito

**[00:55:56.080] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000616)  
sa ating mga ordinaryong mamamayan.

**[00:56:03.740] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000617)  
Madam Speaker,

**[00:56:04.800] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000618)  
Distinguished Colleague,

**[00:56:06.340] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000619)  
the recommendation of the Department of Finance

**[00:56:08.680] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000620)  
will probably reach the Office of the President

**[00:56:11.320] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000621)  
within this week

**[00:56:14.380] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000622)  
and most likely

**[00:56:16.240] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000623)  
we will have an announcement

**[00:56:17.980] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000624)  
early next week.

**[00:56:20.540] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000625)  
And aside from that, Madam Speaker,

**[00:56:22.400] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000626)  
Distinguished Sponsor,

**[00:56:23.700] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000627)  
marami rin pongiente

**[00:56:24.860] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000628)  
ginagawang assistance at tulong ang Office of the President sa mga critical sectors na naapektuhan nitong krisis sa Eneria.

**[00:56:34.340] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000629)  
Sa ating mga magsasaka, transport groups, fisher folks, and most vulnerable members of our communities.

**[00:56:43.100] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000630)  
Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleagues.

**[00:56:45.200] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000631)  
Madam Speaker, hindi po lahat ay nabibigyan ng tulong, hindi po lahat ay nabibigyan ng ayuda,

**[00:56:50.320] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000632)  
maski po ang mga binibigay natin ng subsidies ay kulang na kulang para po sa ating mga drivers, para sa ating mga magsasaka, mga mangingisda.

**[00:57:00.240] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000633)  
Kaya po ang pagsususpindi sa totoo lang ng excise tax sa LPG at kerosene ay isang welcome,

**[00:57:06.500] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000634)  
ngunit hindi pa po talaga sapata na hakbang sa ngayon.

**[00:57:10.520] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000635)  
Hindi nito tinutugunan ang ugat ng krisis.

**[00:57:13.160] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000636)  
Kapag tumaas ang presyo ng langis, bumaba ba ang kita ng mga driver,

**[00:57:16.980] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000637)  
hindi po makapagtanim ang mga magsasaka.

**[00:57:19.600] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000638)  
Hindi makapamalakaya ang mga mangingisda.

**[00:57:23.400] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000639)  
Bumaba ba o wala na pong halaga o wala na talaga silang kita habang tumataas ang presyo ng bawat produkto sa pamilihan.

**[00:57:33.380] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000640)  
Sa ngayon nga po, nag-anunsyo na ng mga transport strike ang ating mga jeepney driver.

**[00:57:39.280] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000641)  
Sunod-sunod din po ang mga pahayag ng mga provincial at city buses

**[00:57:43.580] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000642)  
dahil po lubos talaga na affected ang land transport.

**[00:57:47.700] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000643)  
Kung ang air at sea at transport nakakapag-single po ng mga fuel surcharges,

**[00:57:53.260] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000644)  
hindi po ganyan ang sitwasyon ng ating mga provincial at city buses

**[00:57:59.040] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000645)  
at lubos na po silang umaaray sa napakataas na presyo ng langis.

**[00:58:05.540] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000646)  
Hindi po sila humihingi lamang ng relief,

**[00:58:07.860] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000647)  
nananawagan po talaga sila ng napakalawak na pagbabawas ng pasanin dahil sa mataas na presyo ng langis.

**[00:58:16.600] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000648)  
Dapat po gamitin ng Pangulo yung emergency powers na mabilis din na ipasa dito sa Kongreso

**[00:58:23.160] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000649)  
na naibigay sa kanya upang suspendihin ang excess taxes sa lahat ng produktong petrolyo,

**[00:58:29.640] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000650)  
hindi lamang sa LPG at kerosene.

**[00:58:32.060] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000651)  
Higit pa rito, nananawagan tayo sa kapulungan na ito na agad nang ipasa ang presyo ibaba-bill

**[00:58:37.680] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000652)  
at marami pa pong mga panukala na matagal nang nananawagan sa kapulungan na ito

**[00:58:43.240] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000653)  
na alisin ang VAT sa pangunahing bilihin.

**[00:58:46.600] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000654)  
Sa pagkain, sa kuryente at langis.

**[00:58:49.260] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000655)  
Ang mataas na presyo ng bilihin, kuryente at langis,

**[00:58:52.260] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000656)  
ito po yung tunay na nararanasan na national emergency ng ating mga kababayan.

**[00:58:59.260] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000657)  
Ngayon naman po, hayaan niyo ako na magpunta sa usapin ng napakababang sahod sa ating bayan.

**[00:59:06.260] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000658)  
Isang ayon po sa naiulat dito ng DepDev ng PSA na official poverty threshold ay mas mababa po

**[00:59:16.060] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000659)  
ang poverty threshold doon sa mga minimum wages.

**[00:59:23.400] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000660)  
O mas mababa yung mga minimum wages sa labing dalawang lehyon sa ating poverty threshold.

**[00:59:28.080] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000661)  
Ibig sabihin, hindi na lang po talaga sapat na isang manggagawa lang o isa lang yung may kita, may minimum wage.

**[00:59:36.140] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000662)  
Para makatugon sa pangangailangan ng kanyang buong pamilya, dapat po meron pang dagdag na trabaho

**[00:59:42.780] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000663)  
o nagahanap sila ng ibang mga racket at mga mapagkakataon.

**[00:59:46.060] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000664)  
O kaya naman dapat dalawa o tatlo pa yung nagtutulungan para mabuhay lang ang kanilang buong pamilya.

**[00:59:53.060] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000665)  
Ang tanong ko po sa Office of the President ay meron po ba kayong pag-endorso?

**[01:00:00.000] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000666)  
higil sa pagtatatag halimbawa ng isang pambansang minimum na sahod na nakalapat sa living wages

**[01:00:06.880] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000667)  
o yung sapat para po mabuhay ng may dignidad, ng may nutrisyon, ang isang buong pamilya.

**[01:00:17.420] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000668)  
Before I answer the questions, the question of our distinguished colleague,

**[01:00:22.500] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000669)  
gusto ko lang pong ipahayag sa ating distinguished colleague na I have in front of me a graph

**[01:00:29.720] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000670)  
nung line chart ng wage increase vis-a-vis the inflation index.

**[01:00:37.180] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000671)  
And we will submit this to our distinguished colleague.

**[01:00:41.860] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000672)  
Dito po sa chart na ito, comparably, mas mataas ang tinaas ng ating minimum wage versus the increase ng ating inflation.

**[01:00:57.200] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000673)  
So, palaki ng palaki yung gap.

**[01:00:59.720] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000674)  
Through the years and more so under the administration of President Marcos Jr.

**[01:01:06.700] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000675)  
We will submit this, Madam Speaker, distinguished colleague.

**[01:01:12.140] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000676)  
Madam Speaker, maraming salamat sa ulat na isusumitin sa representasyon na ito.

**[01:01:18.040] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000677)  
Ngunit tayo pa rin po ay lubos na nababahala na yung minimum wage ay literal na mas mababa

**[01:01:23.880] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000678)  
dun sa official na poverty threshold na naisumitin naman sa atin ang DepDev at PSA.

**[01:01:29.720] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000679)  
Nga po, nag-sabi din tayo sa kanila na sa tingin natin hindi pa rin po makatotohanan yung nilalagay po natin na poverty threshold sa ngayon.

**[01:01:40.680] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000680)  
Yung sa loob ng NCR, nasa 26 pesos lang po kada meal or kada pagkain or nasa ganun lang po na halaga.

**[01:01:51.680] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000681)  
At sa labas ng NCR, nasa 22 to 23 pesos kada meal.

**[01:01:55.600] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000682)  
Sabi po na ating mga manggagawa,

**[01:01:57.760] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000683)  
ni pambili po ng pagkain sa mga karinderiya o paghanda ng kanilang mga pagkain sa araw-araw,

**[01:02:03.860] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000684)  
kulang na kulang po yung nasa 22 to 26 pesos na kita para po makasapat sa kanilang mga buong pamilya.

**[01:02:15.020] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000685)  
Kaya po, nananatili na ang panawagan ng mga manggagawa,

**[01:02:18.500] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000686)  
dapat seryoso na talakayin ang pagkakaroon ng pambansang minimum na sahod

**[01:02:22.920] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000687)  
at dapat nakalapat ito sa nakabubuhay sa kanilang mga pambansang minimum.

**[01:02:27.760] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000688)  
mga pamilya o mga living wages na ngayon ay umabot na sa nasa 1,200 to 1,300 kada araw.

**[01:02:35.840] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000689)  
Ngayon naman, Madam Speaker, tungkol po sa panawagan ng mga pamilya,

**[01:02:41.540] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000690)  
mga taga-suporta ni Mary Jane Veloso para sa pag-agrant ng Executive Clemency para sa kanya.

**[01:02:49.280] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000691)  
Nananatili po siyang nakakulong hanggang ngayon, Madam Speaker,

**[01:02:52.920] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000692)  
simula po nang makabalik siya dito sa Pilipinas.

**[01:02:57.160] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000693)  
Siya po ay nananatili sa Correctional Institution for Women.

**[01:03:03.120] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000694)  
Ang kanyang kaso po ay kinilala, hindi lang po dito sa Pilipinas,

**[01:03:07.900] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000695)  
ngunit sa buong mundo na isang malinaw na halimbawa ng human trafficking at forced drug mule exploitation.

**[01:03:16.700] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000696)  
Ano po ba ang tiyak na hakbang na ginagawa ng Office of the President

**[01:03:21.360] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000697)  
upang matiyak?

**[01:03:22.920] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000698)  
Ang kanyang agarang paglaya at matiyak po na makasama niya na po ang kanyang pamilya

**[01:03:29.200] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000699)  
na sa ngayon ay nasa labing-anim na taon na po na siyang nawalay sa kanyang pamilya.

**[01:03:35.600] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000700)  
Yes, Madam Speaker, distinguished colleagues,

**[01:03:38.580] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000701)  
the Office of the President is seriously considering to grant absolute pardon to Mary Jane Veloso

**[01:03:46.840] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000702)  
on humanitarian grounds in support of the government's commitment to protecting women.

**[01:03:52.920] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000703)  
Filipino migrant workers.

**[01:03:56.800] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000704)  
Maraming salamat, Madam Speaker.

**[01:03:58.740] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000705)  
Kailan po natin maaasahan matatapos itong seryosong konsiderasyon?

**[01:04:03.700] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000706)  
Kahigit isang dekada na po na nawalay si Mary Jane sa kanyang pamilya,

**[01:04:10.700] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000707)  
sa kanya pong mga magulang na matanda na ngayon.

**[01:04:14.200] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000708)  
Yung dating niyang mga anak na mga bata pa ngayon po ay malalaki na rin.

**[01:04:20.600] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000709)  
Dami na pong taon.

**[01:04:21.820] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000710)  
Ang nanakaw sa kanya na dahil dito sa malaking inyustisya na kanyang naranasan.

**[01:04:29.180] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000711)  
Madam Speaker, distinguished colleague, there will be an announcement in a few days.

**[01:04:36.940] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000712)  
Maraming salamat, Madam Speaker.

**[01:04:39.700] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000713)  
Magandang balita po ito para kay Mary Jane Veloso sa kanyang pamilya.

**[01:04:45.520] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000714)  
At sana po, ang issue po kasi na ito ay hindi lang tungkol kay Mary Jane Veloso,

**[01:04:50.800] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000715)  
kundi para sa bawat Pilipino na naghahangad ng mas magandang buhay,

**[01:04:56.120] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000716)  
ng disenteng trabaho dito sa ating bansa.

**[01:05:00.780] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000717)  
At dapat may kalakip ito na pagpapalakas din ng ating laban sa trafficking,

**[01:05:07.560] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000718)  
pagpapalakas ng ating proteksyon para sa mga migrant workers,

**[01:05:12.060] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000719)  
at higit sa lahat, pagtitiyak ng aktibo na paglikha ng mas maraming trabaho dito sa Pilipinas.

**[01:05:19.820] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000720)  
Trabaho na may nakabubuhay na sahod para sa kanilang mga pamilya.

**[01:05:26.680] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000721)  
At panghuli, Madam Speaker, tungkol po sa Marawi Rehabilitation and Development.

**[01:05:34.900] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000722)  
Sa ilalim ng Executive Order No. 78, nilikha ang Office of the Presidential Advisor for Marawi Rehabilitation and Development

**[01:05:43.160] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000723)  
para sa coordination at integration ng lahat ng government programs para sa rehabilitation at development.

**[01:05:49.820] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000724)  
At kasama rito ang housing at resettlement. Tama po ba?

**[01:05:57.000] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000725)  
At sa kisalukuyan, Madam Speaker, ang disyud nung nagpunta po siya dito sa plenaryo,

**[01:06:04.460] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000726)  
sinabi niya na wala nang nakalaan ni PISO para sa direktang pagtatayo ng bagong permanent housing

**[01:06:12.540] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000727)  
para sa mga Marawi internally displaced person.

**[01:06:16.100] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000728)  
At mas inaasahan na ang LGUs na mangunguna sa permanent housing.

**[01:06:24.720] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000731)  
Kung ngayon, Madam Speaker, pwede ba tayong balitaan ng ating distinguished sponsor?

**[01:06:28.300] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000732)  
Ano ba ang magiging papel nitong OPA-MRD sa pagkiyak na may permanent housing para sa mga pamilya

**[01:06:38.000] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000733)  
na hindi pa po nakakabalik sa Marawi?

**[01:06:41.040] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000734)  
O at yung mga naninirahan pa rin magpasa hanggang ngayon sa Marawi sa mga temporary shelters?

**[01:06:48.420] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000735)  
Madam Speaker,

**[01:06:49.820] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000736)  
Distinguished Colleagues,

**[01:06:51.420] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000737)  
Napakagandang development po na kakatapos lang po ng BARM elections nitong nakaraang linggo

**[01:06:58.640] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000738)  
and with political stability in the area and with the support of the local officials that are newly elected

**[01:07:09.720] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000739)  
and who received fresh mandate from their people in coordination with the national government and the president.

**[01:07:19.820] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000740)  
Siguradong mas mapapabilis na po ngayon ang development sa Marawi.

**[01:07:25.460] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000741)  
Magtutulong na po yung locality, yung local officials at sigurado naman po susuportahan po ito ng Office of the President

**[01:07:33.380] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000742)  
para once and for all matapos na po yung inaasam-asam nating development sa Marawi.

**[01:07:39.980] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000743)  
Especially yung ating housing na matagal na pong ginagawa dito sa lugar na ito.

**[01:07:46.180] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000744)  
Madam Speaker, sa ilalim din ang Section 4 ng EO 78.

**[01:07:49.820] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000745)  
Inatasan ang implementing agencies na mag-submit sa OPA-MRD ng status ng kanilang programs, activities, and projects,

**[01:08:00.520] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000746)  
causes of delay, catch-up plans, at inventory ng assets at accountabilities.

**[01:08:05.700] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000747)  
Mayroon na po ba siya ngayon na consolidated report ang OPA-MRD?

**[01:08:11.340] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000748)  
Lalong-lalo na po dito sa usapin ng permanent housing at big test na pagbabalik ng ating Marawi IDPs.

**[01:08:19.820] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000749)  
Sa kanila pong mga lugar, sa kanila pong mga area kung saan po sila original na nakatira.

**[01:08:28.200] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000750)  
Maari po ba nating maisumite sa kapulungan na ito dahil mahalaga po nabantayan natin at huwag po natin kalimutan

**[01:08:35.660] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000751)  
na marami pa po tayong Marawi IDPs ang nananawagan ng karapatan po nila sa pagbabalik sa kanilang mga tahanan.

**[01:08:47.460] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000752)  
Yes, Madam Speaker, distinguished colleagues.

**[01:08:49.920] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000753)  
There is a consolidated report and we will submit.

**[01:08:56.760] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000754)

**[01:15:15.440] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000837)

**[01:15:45.440] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000840)

**[01:15:52.540] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000841)

**[01:16:08.020] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-000842)

**[01:23:30.060] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000983)

**[01:23:30.880] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000984)  
ay para sa

**[01:23:32.400] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000985)

**[01:23:35.200] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000986)  
ng 115

**[01:23:38.040] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000987)  
million

**[01:23:39.120] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000988)  
Filipinos.

iyong binanggit ng ating kasama

**[01:23:47.740] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000992)

**[01:23:59.660] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000995)

**[01:24:04.500] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000996)

**[01:24:10.400] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-000998)  
ng ating budget to protect the Filipino people.

and inaudit.

**[01:25:05.180] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001006)  
Intelligence Fund, Madam Speaker.

**[01:27:36.780] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001031)

**[01:41:34.980] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001176)

**[01:41:54.960] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-001178)

**[01:55:25.300] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001314)  
Iyon lamang po.

**[01:55:27.400] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001315)  
Chair.

**[01:55:41.180] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001316)  
Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague,

**[01:55:44.340] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001317)  
marami pong ginagawa ang ating Pangulo para umangat po yung ating ekonomiya.

**[01:55:50.300] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001318)  
And of course, number one dito, yung pinaka-importanting legislation na ating ginagawa taon-taon,

**[01:55:57.400] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001319)  
which is the timely and quality passage of our budget.

**[01:56:03.940] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001320)  
Ang laki ng tulong po nito para sa government spending on time by the turn of the year,

**[01:56:11.860] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001321)  
nang sa ganoon ay lalo pang umangat ang ating GDP.

**[01:56:16.660] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001322)  
At the same time, nakita naman po natin na napaka-busy po ng ating Pangulo

**[01:56:22.040] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001323)  
in trying to attract foreign direct investment.

**[01:56:26.520] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001324)  
Okay.

**[01:56:27.400] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001325)  
Nung isang linggo, nasa India po siya.

**[01:56:30.440] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001326)  
And there were commitments made by big businesses in India to invest in the Philippines.

**[01:56:38.400] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001327)  
Kahapon, nakita po natin yung trade agreement signed by our Secretary of DTI with the EU,

**[01:56:46.880] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001328)  
which is, if I'm not mistaken, the third biggest economy in the world.

**[01:56:53.340] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001329)  
At mukhang susunod pa po ang isa pang trade agreement with Canada.

**[01:56:57.400] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001330)  
So, sa North America naman po, ito.

**[01:57:01.080] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001331)  
So, aside from government spending and attraction of foreign direct investment,

**[01:57:08.060] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001332)  
hopefully, pag malampasan po natin itong energy crisis na ating pinagdadaanan

**[01:57:13.740] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001333)  
at ginagawan din po ng paraan ng ating Pangulo para maibsan itong crisis na ito

**[01:57:19.240] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001334)  
sa pinagdadaanan ng ating mahamayan,

**[01:57:23.500] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001335)  
hopefully, tumaas na po at manumbalik yung sigla.

**[01:57:27.400] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001336)  
Nang ating GDP.

**[01:57:40.160] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001337)  
And the Office of the President is confident with the timely passage of this budget,

**[01:57:46.300] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001338)  
babalik na tayo dun sa ating trajectory nung normal growth natin before the crisis began,

**[01:57:52.260] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001339)  
which is around 5, 5.5 to 6 percent.

**[01:57:56.700] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001340)  
ng ating GDP.

**[01:57:59.040] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001342)  
And this will, of course, create a lot of jobs and opportunities for our country.

**[01:58:23.440] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001343)  
With regards to the management of our debt,

**[01:58:26.360] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001344)  
Madam Speaker,

**[01:58:27.960] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001346)  
Honorable Colleague,

**[01:58:30.220] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001347)  
pababa po ng pababa ang ating debt to GDP ratio.

**[01:58:35.200] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001348)  
A deficit to GDP ratio.

**[01:58:39.440] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001349)  
So, from 5.6 last year to 5.4 this year,

**[01:58:52.260] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001350)  
and 4.8 by 2028.

**[01:58:55.580] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001351)  
So, pababa po yung...

**[01:58:57.400] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001352)  
So, pababa po yung deficit to GDP ratio.

**[01:59:00.880] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001353)  
And we will maintain this trajectory para bumaba nang bumaba yung ratio.

**[01:59:13.080] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001354)  
And because of that, Madam Speaker, Honorable Colleague,

**[01:59:17.760] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001355)  
by 2028, bababa na rin po yung debt to GDP ratio.

**[01:59:23.560] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001356)  
Salamat po.

**[01:59:24.560] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001357)  
Yung pong reform agenda natin, yung kadena.

**[01:59:26.740] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001358)  
Yeah.

**[01:59:26.760] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001359)  
With regards to the Kadena Act, sinimula na po ng BBM.

**[01:59:33.160] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001360)  
Kung napapansin po ninyo yung kanyang programang entitled COMPASS,

**[01:59:39.800] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001361)  
or Centralized Open Monitoring Platform for Appropriation and Spending Statistics.

**[01:59:47.960] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001362)  
Ito po yung nasa website ng BBM.

**[01:59:51.400] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001363)  
At nakita po po ito personally.

**[01:59:54.360] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001364)  
Very informative.

**[01:59:55.640] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001365)  
And this is open.

**[01:59:56.760] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001366)  
To house members and everyone.

**[02:00:00.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001367)  
para makita po natin yung spending ng ating gobyerno.

**[02:00:04.380] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001368)  
And this is the first step to Kadena.

**[02:00:06.960] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001369)  
Because once we digitalize, we now have the speed of light.

**[02:00:12.280] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001370)  
Kumbaga, kahit sino pwede mag-download, pwede natin i-apply ang AI dito to analyze.

**[02:00:18.920] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001371)  
At kapag napasa na po ng both houses of Congress ang Kadena Act,

**[02:00:22.980] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001372)  
mas madali pong i-plug in dahil digitalize na po ang spending ng ating gobyerno.

**[02:00:28.640] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001373)  
And this is my point, Mr. Speaker, that there's a pending bill in the House and the Senate regarding the Kadena Act.

**[02:00:39.400] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001374)  
So, ito po ba ay idideklara na urgent ng ating Pangulo?

**[02:00:48.300] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001375)  
Yes. The position of the President, he is supportive of this legislation.

**[02:00:55.880] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001376)  
In fact, Madam Speaker, Honorable Colleague,

**[02:00:58.640] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001377)  
nasa ledak po itong legislation na ito.

**[02:01:03.680] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001378)  
Kasama po siya sa isa, sa priority, legislative, executive priorities.

**[02:01:11.220] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001379)  
Naipasa na rin po yung anti-political dynasty version ng House

**[02:01:15.540] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001380)  
at naipasa na rin po sa second reading, ang sa Senate.

**[02:01:21.260] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001381)  
So, may balak po ba ang Pangulo na maideclare na urgent ang anti-political dynasty?

**[02:01:28.640] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001382)  
Yes. Madam Speaker, Honorable Colleague,

**[02:01:38.560] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001383)  
I am quoting our Executive Secretary na he would like to take this opportunity

**[02:01:44.680] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001384)  
to address the House of Representatives and the Senate to pass this legislation.

**[02:01:53.920] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001385)  
The President is very supportive of this bill.

**[02:01:57.000] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001386)  
Madam Speaker, Honorable Colleague.

**[02:01:58.640] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001387)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[02:02:01.200] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001388)  
And for my last question,

**[02:02:03.340] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001389)  
basura, trapiko, pabahay at land use ang naguugnay na problema sa Metro Manila.

**[02:02:09.780] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001390)  
36% po ng GDP natin galing dito.

**[02:02:13.660] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001391)  
At kailangan-kailangan pong mabigyan ng solusyon.

**[02:02:16.960] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001392)  
Bukas po ba ang Pangulo na supportahan ng isang elected Metropolitan Manila Government

**[02:02:22.660] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001393)  
sa ilalim ng Article 10, Section 11 ng ating Constitution

**[02:02:26.260] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001394)  
na nagpapahintulot sa Congress?

**[02:02:28.640] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001395)  
So, na lumika ng Special Metropolitan Political Subdivision

**[02:02:33.160] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001396)  
nasa sa ilalim sa isang plebisito?

**[02:02:37.880] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001397)  
Madam Speaker, Honorable Colleague,

**[02:02:40.980] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001398)  
ang ating Executive Secretary have not discussed this particular issue with the President,

**[02:02:48.420] Rep. Albert S. Garcia** (asr-001399)  
pero according to our ES, he thinks this is a good idea.

**[02:02:54.380] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001400)  
Salamat po.

**[02:02:54.880] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001401)  
In this case, in behalf of the minority,

**[02:02:57.760] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001402)  
I move.

**[02:02:58.380] Rep. Edgar R. Erice** (asr-001403)

**[02:09:59.380] Rep. Ma. Alana Samantha Taliño Santos** (asr-001466)  
Alinig...

Alianigue, Office of In Charge Arlene Barrientos, Director Nicole Bacolod, Office of In Charge Jericho Paloma, Assistant Director General Christine Bennett Andal,

**[02:10:23.640] Rep. Ma. Alana Samantha Taliño Santos** (asr-001468)

**[02:10:28.380] Rep. Ma. Alana Samantha Taliño Santos** (asr-001469)

**[02:10:41.520] Deputy Speaker Francisco Paolo P. Ortega V** (asr-001470)

**[02:10:45.680] Deputy Speaker Francisco Paolo P. Ortega V** (asr-001471)  
led by Ad interim Secretary Dave M. Gomez,

**[02:10:49.340] Deputy Speaker Francisco Paolo P. Ortega V** (asr-001472)  
welcome to the House of Representatives.

**[02:10:52.900] Deputy Speaker Francisco Paolo P. Ortega V** (asr-001473)  
Majority Leader?

**[02:10:55.760] Rep. Ma. Alana Samantha Taliño Santos** (asr-001474)  
Mr. Speaker?

**[02:10:58.380] Rep. Ma. Alana Samantha Taliño Santos** (asr-001475)  
I move that we recognize Vice Chairperson Queenie Gonzales of the Lone District of Mandaluyong to sponsor the proposed budget of the Presidential Communications Office.

**[02:11:18.420] Deputy Speaker Francisco Paolo P. Ortega V** (asr-001476)  
The Honorable Gonzales is now recognized. You may proceed.

**[02:11:23.500] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-001477)  
Mr. Speaker, distinguished colleagues, good morning.

**[02:11:28.380] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-001487)  
It is an honor and privilege to sponsor the proposed 2.62 billion budget of the Presidential Communications Office

**[02:11:35.720] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-001488)  
and its attached agencies and corporations for fiscal year 2027.

**[02:11:42.320] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-001489)  
As the lead communications arm of the government,

**[02:11:46.160] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-001490)  
the PCO plays a vital role in ensuring that the Filipino people receive timely, accurate, accessible,

**[02:11:53.300] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-001491)  
and responsible information on government policies, programs,

**[02:11:57.940] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-001492)  
and services.

**[02:11:58.940] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-001493)  
We have the lowest budget and the last department-level agency of the Executive Department in terms of budgetary allocation.

**[02:12:08.180] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-001494)  
PCO may have the least budget priority, but they assure your honors that they are up to the Herculean task of engaging and involving the citizenry and the mass media in enriching the quality of public discourse on all matters of governance.

**[02:12:27.540] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-001495)  
This function is essential to good governance.

**[02:12:30.920] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-001496)  
Government programs can serve the public only when citizens understand what assistance is available, how to access it, why policies are being adopted, and how public institutions may be held accountable.

**[02:12:46.060] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-001497)  
Clear communication, therefore, connects policy with public service, supports participation, and helps sustain trust between the government and the people.

**[02:12:54.780] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-001498)  
This responsibility has become

**[02:12:57.520] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-001499)  
even more significant at a time when social media is woven into everyday life.

**[02:13:03.960] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-001500)  
Information now reaches millions within seconds.

**[02:13:08.400] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-001501)  
But so do misinformation, disinformation, manipulated content, and AI-generated material.

**[02:13:17.160] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-001502)  
The PCO must be present where people receive and exchange information,

**[02:13:34.420] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-001505)

**[02:13:47.060] Rep. Alexandria "Queenie" Gonzales** (asr-001506)

**[02:23:27.080] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-001545)  
The Honorable Vice Chair Monique Yasmin Maria Lagdameo is hereby recognized to sponsor the proposed budget of the Commission on Higher Education.

**[02:23:35.520] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-001546)  
Thank you, Madam Speaker. It is my distinct privilege to sponsor the proposed fiscal year 2027 budget of the Commission on Higher Education, amounting to P35.45 billion, inclusive of the automatic appropriations.

**[02:23:48.760] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-001547)  
It is a decrease of 26.7% or P12.9 billion, the current level of P48.27 billion.

**[02:23:56.900] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-001548)  
Mandated to promote accessible, quality and relevant

**[02:24:00.000] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-001549)  
higher education in the country, the proposed budget will continue to support CHED's efforts to strengthen higher education institutions,

**[02:24:08.600] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-001550)  
provide opportunities for deserving students, promote research and innovation,

**[02:24:13.580] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-001551)  
and ensure that our colleges and universities remain responsive to the evolving needs of our country and our people.

**[02:24:20.600] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-001552)  
CHED's mandate also encompasses the development of higher education plans and policies,

**[02:24:27.160] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-001553)  
the establishment of standards,

**[02:24:28.920] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-001554)  
and the administration of the Higher Education Development Fund.

**[02:24:33.840] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-001555)  
We must continue to expand access while protecting the quality of education,

**[02:24:38.400] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-001556)  
strengthen scholarships and student assistance,

**[02:24:40.580] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-001557)  
support research, innovation, and emerging fields of study,

**[02:24:44.340] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-001558)  
and address disparities and gaps in educational opportunities across regions.

**[02:24:51.320] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-001559)  
Our obligation as an institution is clear to provide the resources necessary to build a higher education system...

**[02:24:59.000] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-001560)  
...that is...

**[02:24:59.500] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-001561)  
...accessible to all, competitive with the changing world, and responsive to the demands of an evolving economy.

**[02:25:06.500] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-001562)  
With these objectives, this representation respectfully submits the proposed fiscal year 2027 budget of the Commission on Higher Education for consideration and urges its body to approve the same.

**[03:21:59.760] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002678)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker.

**[03:22:02.820] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002679)  
Mr. Speaker, marami pong katanungan natin ay mas appropriate na itanong para sa SUC budget.

**[03:22:13.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002680)  
So I will ask them later.

**[03:22:15.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002681)  
So a few questions regarding the policies of CHED.

**[03:22:21.560] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002682)  
Of course, alam natin na katulong ang CHED.

**[03:22:27.620] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002683)  
Madalas po ay katulong ang CHED sa laban para itaas ang budget,

**[03:22:35.240] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002684)  
lalo na sa ating mga state universities and colleges,

**[03:22:37.980] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002685)  
at para sa higher education in general.

**[03:22:41.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002686)  
Ngayon, I just have a few.

**[03:22:43.540] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002687)  
At isa sa mga advokasya po,

**[03:22:45.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002688)  
ng CHED ngayon ay yung pag-develop ng isang composite fee,

**[03:22:52.800] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002689)  
parang standard composite fee for state universities and colleges.

**[03:23:01.360] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002690)  
Iba-iba po talaga yung mga tuition fee rates or composite fee rates

**[03:23:09.220] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002691)  
that the more, the 100, ilan na ba tayo?

**[03:23:13.840] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002692)  
113 or 160?

**[03:23:15.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002693)  
State universities and colleges charge.

**[03:23:20.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002694)  
So ayon sa datos,

**[03:23:22.220] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002695)  
there are five state universities that charge 50 pesos or less per unit,

**[03:23:30.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002696)  
including several in Metro Manila and the Zamboanga Peninsula.

**[03:23:34.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002697)  
Tapos 32 SUCs charge from 51 pesos to 100 pesos per unit.

**[03:23:39.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002698)  
Then 101 pesos to 200 pesos per unit,

**[03:23:44.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002699)  
57 SUCs.

**[03:23:45.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002700)  
201 pesos to 1,000 per unit,

**[03:23:51.520] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002701)  
19 SUCs.

**[03:23:53.280] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002702)  
So why does this matter?

**[03:23:54.620] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002703)  
This matters because of the,

**[03:23:57.980] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002704)  
consequently, the varying levels of subsidy provided to state universities and colleges.

**[03:24:05.900] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002705)  
So napakalawak po ng range.

**[03:24:10.820] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002706)  
Ngayon, pwede po bang magbigay ng update ang CHED?

**[03:24:15.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002707)  
On the work that they have been doing to standardize composite fees

**[03:24:23.920] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002708)  
as a basis for subsidies to state universities and colleges, among others.

**[03:24:32.320] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002709)  
Ano pong status?

**[03:24:40.560] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-002712)  
Yes.

**[03:24:45.020] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-002717)  
Part of the SGV study, this was included also.

**[03:24:49.760] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-002718)  
And so, with the results of that, the CHED now has many options as its proposal for the different SUCs,

**[03:25:01.900] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-002719)  
depending on kung ano po yung mapag-usapan na kakayanin po per SUC.

**[03:25:06.780] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-002720)  
Pero marami naman po silang ginawang options as proposals po.

**[03:25:12.620] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002721)  
Ano po yung proposals or options?

**[03:25:15.020] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002722)  
Para malinaw din po sa plenaryo.

**[03:25:18.060] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002723)  
No?

**[03:25:44.680] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-002739)  
Sige po.

**[03:25:50.380] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-002740)  
So, if based on SGV, we will be needing 53.7B.

**[03:25:56.800] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-002741)  
If based on DBM, we will be needing 29.6B.

**[03:26:02.300] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-002742)  
And based on CHED's proposal, we will be needing 4.2.

**[03:26:06.400] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-002743)  
So, in the meantime, knowing na lahat po yun na malaking figures,

**[03:26:10.240] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-002744)  
meron din pong proposal ang CHED for a leveling up scheme.

**[03:26:17.320] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002745)  
Yes, could we hear this leveling up scheme proposed by CHED?

**[03:26:25.100] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002746)  
Napakahalaga po na malaman ng kongresong ito para baka tugon.

**[03:26:30.120] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002747)  
If ever, leveling up, ibig sabihin pagdagdag,

**[03:26:34.220] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002748)  
step-by-step na pagdagdag sa subsidy sa mga state universities.

**[03:26:39.260] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-002749)  
Sige po.

**[03:26:40.240] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-002750)  
If it's for SUCs that charge below 250 per unit,

**[03:26:49.460] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-002751)  
there are 106 of them, then there is a need for 4.2B.

**[03:26:56.320] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002752)  
Okay.

**[03:26:59.420] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002753)  
Meron pa po bang additional info?

**[03:27:02.640] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002754)  
So, yun yung pinaka...

**[03:27:03.480] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-002755)  
For those, for the seven suks who charge 250 and above,

**[03:27:09.280] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-002756)  
there is a funding needed of 87.4 million.

**[03:27:20.560] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-002757)  
But yung second po, medyo hindi po okay sa pasok.

**[03:27:25.740] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002758)  
Okay.

**[03:27:26.300] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-002759)  
Oo.

**[03:27:27.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002760)  
Okay.

**[03:27:28.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002761)  
Alright.

**[03:27:29.360] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002762)  
So, parang ganito po, para malinaw.

**[03:27:32.640] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002763)  
So, sa ngayon, ay binabatay,

**[03:27:37.640] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002764)  
nabatay, isang batayan ng BBM para sa subsidy sa mga state universities and colleges

**[03:27:45.460] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002765)  
ay yung composite fee rate.

**[03:27:48.420] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002766)  
Tama po ba?

**[03:27:49.700] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-002767)  
Yes, po.

**[03:27:50.140] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002768)  
No?

**[03:27:51.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002769)  
And in the IRR of the Republic Act 10931, the free, yung UACTEA,

**[03:27:59.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002770)  
Universal Access to Quality Tertiary Education,

**[03:28:02.800] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002771)  
composite fee refers to an index consisting of a single fee

**[03:28:06.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002772)  
or a range of three.

**[03:28:07.640] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002773)  
So, it's a range of fees that aggregates the basic costs of teaching

**[03:28:11.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002774)  
and other fees incidental or related to instruction

**[03:28:14.300] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002775)  
based on the normative cost of a given program.

**[03:28:18.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002776)  
So, tinatakda yan o dinidetermine yan ng bawat board ng mga state university.

**[03:28:26.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002777)  
Sabi nila, ito yung cost, normative cost of program sa aming school.

**[03:28:31.660] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002778)  
Kung nursing, meron ganitong fee.

**[03:28:33.920] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002779)  
Kung education, ganito, and so on and so forth.

**[03:28:36.760] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002780)  
Okay.

**[03:28:37.640] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002781)  
Ito ang isang basis para sa subsidy na binibigay sa state university

**[03:28:43.240] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002782)  
para ma-implement yung free higher education.

**[03:28:46.900] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002783)  
Ngayon, katulad ng nabanggit kanina, iba-iba, wide range.

**[03:28:50.980] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002784)  
Merong below 50 pesos per unit, merong below 250 pesos per unit and so on.

**[03:28:57.500] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002785)  
Merong iba na state universities, 1,000 pesos per unit ang china-charge.

**[03:29:03.700] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002786)  
So, iba-iba rin yung subsidy.

**[03:29:05.260] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002787)  
So, ang proposal na nga po, at tinatakda,

**[03:29:07.640] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002788)  
yung mga trabaho, and we appreciate the work being done by CHED under CHERA groupies,

**[03:29:12.440] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002789)  
they're coming up with a standardized normative cost.

**[03:29:16.740] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002790)  
And naturally, ang pinakamakikinabang dito, yung mga mababa, yung per unit cost na sinisingel.

**[03:29:24.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002791)  
Diba?

**[03:29:25.520] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002792)  
At dapat lang naman, kasi many of those fees, yung mga below 50, parang hindi na na-raise yan

**[03:29:33.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002793)  
for understandable reasons.

**[03:29:35.620] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002794)  
Sa state university, ayaw mo mag-raise.

**[03:29:37.640] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002795)  
Ang pinakamakikinabang dito, ayaw mo mag-raise ng tuition fee.

**[03:29:39.260] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002796)  
No?

**[03:29:40.000] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002797)  
Okay.

**[03:29:41.140] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002798)  
So, ngayon, ang sinasabi po, may binabanggit kayong SGV.

**[03:29:46.320] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002799)  
Just to clarify for the record, ano ba yung SGV?

**[03:29:55.720] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-002800)  
SGV is the CISEP Gores and Balayo firm.

**[03:29:59.080] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002801)  
Okay.

**[03:30:00.000] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-002802)  
Which has been commissioned by CHED to help them with all these studies.

**[03:30:05.300] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002803)  
Yes. So in other words, kinumission ng CHED, ang SGV, the well-known accounting firm, to come up with a composite fee proposal.

**[03:30:17.280] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002804)  
A standard composite fee proposal. Yun na nga yung binabanggit.

**[03:30:20.640] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002805)  
So, yung sa binanggit niyong minimum scenario, yung composite fee magiging P200 per unit. Tama po ba ako?

**[03:30:33.500] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-002806)  
Hindi po.

**[03:30:34.460] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002807)  
Sige po, pakicorrect.

**[03:30:35.680] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-002808)  
The basis…

**[03:30:37.100] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002809)  
Yung binanggit niyo na minimum P4.2 billion nakakailanganin, ano pong basis nun?

**[03:30:46.760] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-002810)  
Yung P4 billion?

**[03:30:48.540] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002811)  
P4.2 billion, sabi niyo. Proposal.

**[03:30:50.640] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-002812)  
Proposal ng CHED.

**[03:30:52.240] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-002813)  
Leveling up lang po yun.

**[03:30:53.760] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-002814)  
Leveling up.

**[03:30:54.500] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-002815)  
Yes, it's a leveling up.

**[03:30:54.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002816)  
Meaning to say, what would happen?

**[03:30:56.460] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002817)  
What would happen?

**[03:31:03.400] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-002818)  
For SUCS collecting below P250 per unit, they will be a… aangat po silang lahat.

**[03:31:11.220] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-002819)  
Aangat to P250 na po.

**[03:31:14.520] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002820)  
Okay.

**[03:31:15.140] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-002821)  
Yes.

**[03:31:15.460] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002822)  
So, ito. At ilang university ang makikinabang dyan?

**[03:31:19.960] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-002823)  
One hundred seven.

**[03:31:20.640] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002824)  
One hundred seventy of the one hundred sixteen ba? Ano na ba ang latest?

**[03:31:27.580] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-002825)  
One hundred thirteen.

**[03:31:29.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002826)  
Oh, one hundred thirteen. One hundred seven of the one hundred thirteen ay makikinabang.

**[03:31:35.720] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002827)  
So, in other words, kung gagawing P250 per unit na, yung kumbaga standardized composite fee.

**[03:31:46.460] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002828)  
So, maraming ang mayorya.

**[03:31:50.640] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002829)  
Halos lahat.

Okay.

**[03:32:21.160] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-002841)

**[03:32:27.460] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002843)

**[03:32:30.000] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002844)  
Okay. So, basta yun yung point. Gawin ng P250.

**[03:32:33.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002845)  
At para makuha yan, kailangan dagdagan ng P4.2 billion. Tama po?

**[03:32:39.740] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-002846)

**[03:32:42.560] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002847)

So, of course, sinusuportahan po.

**[03:32:49.400] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002849)  
So, na ang kinatawang ito, yung gano'ng pagtaas sa subsidy ng mga state universities and colleges.

**[03:32:58.920] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002850)  
Hiwalay pa to dun sa nire-raise naman ng mga state universities and colleges na free higher education deficiency

**[03:33:06.560] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002851)  
for the years, for the current year, 2026, and projected for 2027.

**[03:33:13.220] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002852)  
Doon ko na lang po babanggitin yun sa deliberation ng SUCS.

**[03:33:19.400] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002853)  
Okay. Ngayon, just a policy question here regarding the use of composite fees.

**[03:33:30.720] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002854)  
Bakit po ba yan ang ginagamit? Anong basis?

**[03:33:36.420] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002855)  
Kasi actually, kung titignan mo yung Republic Act 10931, wala naman na there is no mention whatsoever of composite fees.

**[03:33:44.020] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002856)  
So, it only appears in the IRR.

**[03:33:48.040] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002857)  
And earlier, I already mentioned.

**[03:33:49.400] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002858)  
Or read into the record, the definition of composite fees as defined in the IRR of Republic Act 10931.

**[03:34:03.420] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002859)  
And specifically, para mapabilis po yung discussion natin, yung composite fee ay minandato ang UNIFAS na mag-develop ng composite fee

**[03:34:14.980] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002860)  
as a basis for charging.

**[03:34:18.400] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002861)  
As a basis for charging.

**[03:34:19.400] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002862)  
Those students who are either not qualified or who opt out of the right to free tertiary education.

**[03:34:27.160] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002863)  
Diba? So, kung hindi ka na-qualified or you opt out voluntarily, tisingilin ka.

**[03:34:33.500] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002864)  
At ang singil sa iyo ay yung composite fee na dinetermine ng UNIFAS.

**[03:34:40.840] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002865)  
Tama po ba?

**[03:34:42.840] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002866)  
Yes.

**[03:34:43.480] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002867)  
Okay. Wala. Hindi nakalagay sa either sa 10931.

**[03:34:49.400] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002868)  
Or sa IRR na yung composite fee ang magiging basis para sa subsidy ng mga higher education institutions.

**[03:35:03.920] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002869)  
No?

**[03:35:04.920] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002870)  
It's not. It's nowhere stated there. Pero ngayon, mukhang ganun ang ginagamit.

**[03:35:09.620] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002871)  
Okay. Tama po ba?

**[03:35:13.240] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002872)  
And meron po akong punto rito.

**[03:35:15.300] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002873)  
Pero diba ganun ang nangyayari ngayon?

**[03:35:19.400] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002874)  
As we just discussed, yung composite fee, which is helpful in the sense na magkakaroon ng mas mataas na subsidy

**[03:35:30.460] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002875)  
ang mga state universities and colleges.

**[03:35:34.240] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002876)  
However, my position is, or yung ina-articulate po natin dito, this is inadequate.

**[03:35:42.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002877)  
Hindi sapat.

**[03:35:43.040] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002878)  
Okay. Well, for one,

**[03:35:46.640] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002879)  
I generally have a reservation nang gagaling po sa posisyon, matagal ng posisyon ng ACT at saka ng iba pang mga party list natin sa makabayan.

**[03:36:01.280] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002880)  
And the mass movement in general, ayaw natin ang mag-usap in terms of tuition fees or other fees in relation to free tuition.

**[03:36:16.640] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002881)  
Bakit sinusukat mo in terms of fees?

**[03:36:20.260] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002882)  
Okay. That's one.

**[03:36:21.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002883)  
The other one is,

**[03:36:23.800] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002884)  
the concept of composite fee only measures the current cost of attendance.

**[03:36:31.660] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002885)  
Diba?

**[03:36:32.880] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002886)  
Kung magkano yung kailangan or yung gastos para sa pag-enroll ng isang studyante.

**[03:36:40.760] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002887)  
Okay?

**[03:36:41.060] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002888)  
The problem there is that,

**[03:36:44.480] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002889)  
hindi lang yun ang kailangan.

**[03:36:46.640] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002890)  
Ang mga State Universities and colleges.

**[03:36:48.420] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002891)  
For example,

**[03:36:49.540] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002892)  
a composite fee would not cover the need for expansion or the addressing of infrastructure or personnel or other resource backlogs.

**[03:37:04.520] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002893)  
Kaya nga palaging issue po ng mga State Universities, capital outlay.

**[03:37:09.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002894)  
No?

**[03:37:10.800] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002895)  
Essentially, yung composite fee,

**[03:37:15.400] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002896)  
Current maintenance and operations, yun ang kinukover nun.

**[03:37:21.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002897)  
So yun po ang punto natin na hindi sapat.

**[03:37:26.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002898)  
It's an inadequate tool to support the subsidy required by higher education institutions,

**[03:37:44.900] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002899)  
particular state universities and colleges,

**[03:37:47.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002900)  
to fully deliver on the mandate and fulfill the mandate of Republic Act 10931,

**[03:37:54.520] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002901)  
free higher education.

**[03:37:56.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002902)  
Yun nga ang problema.

**[03:37:59.100] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002903)  
Dahil sa naging libre, lumalaki ang enrollment.

**[03:38:02.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002904)  
At generally, ang policy ng mga state universities natin,

**[03:38:07.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002905)  
hanggapin as much as possible.

**[03:38:09.220] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002906)  
Kaya nga po ngayon, ang lalaki na ng enrollment ng PUP notably,

**[03:38:14.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002907)  
and I'm sure many other state universities and colleges,

**[03:38:19.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002908)  
dumadami.

**[03:38:20.480] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002909)  
And it's a good thing na dumadami ang enrollment sa ating state universities and colleges

**[03:38:26.020] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002910)  
pero hindi nakasasabay yung subsidy ng gobyerno.

**[03:38:29.520] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002911)  
Kailangan, para mabigyan sila ng quality education,

**[03:38:33.100] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002912)  
kailangan may classroom.

**[03:38:34.900] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002913)  
Kailangan may dagdag na teacher.

**[03:38:37.620] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002914)  
At yung teacher hindi pwedeng job order or contract of service.

**[03:38:42.300] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002915)  
Dahil mga highly qualified professionals ang mga ito.

**[03:38:45.760] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002916)  
Pero wala. Bakit wala?

**[03:38:47.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002917)  
Dahil mali po yung instrumentong ginagamit.

**[03:38:51.960] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002918)  
Every year, yan ang problema natin.

**[03:38:55.140] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002919)  
Merong free...

**[03:38:56.020] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002920)  
Free Higher Education Deficit.

**[03:38:58.320] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002921)  
Deficiency.

**[03:39:00.700] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-002922)  
Nasolved natin for fiscal year 2026.

Nagtulungan po ang CHED, Kongreso at PASOK.

At nakapag-appropriate tayo ng additional 12 billion

Ang problema po yung instrumento.

Of RA 10931.

**[03:39:58.680] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-002939)

**[04:03:14.200] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003618)

**[04:03:49.400] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003620)

**[04:03:54.820] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003621)

**[04:04:20.740] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-003622)

**[04:04:24.160] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003623)  
Thank you.

**[04:04:24.820] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003624)

**[04:04:27.520] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003625)

**[04:04:34.300] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003626)  
Para sa mga estudyanteng malayo sa kanilang paralan, umula sa low-income families, maaaring ang gasto sa pamasahe, tirahan, pagkain, books, at internet ang maging dahilan kung bakit nahirapan silang mag-enroll at magpatuloy hanggang makagraduate.

**[04:04:47.940] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003627)  
Mr. Speaker, Madam Sponsor, para sa maraming pamilyang Pilipino, malaking investment ang pag-aaral ng anak.

**[04:04:54.820] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003628)  
Kaya, mahalaga po na ang tulong ng gobyerno mula sa scholarship hanggang sa pag-atid ng quality education ay talagang nakakatulong sa estudyanteng hanggang siya ay makapagtapos at magkaroon ng trabaho.

**[04:05:07.360] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003629)  
Hangad po natin na mas mapalakas pa ng CHED ang mga programa, reforma, at serbisyo na tutugon sa tunay na pangailangan ng mga estudyante at maganda sa kanila para sa maraming oportunidad at magandang kinabukasan.

**[04:05:18.900] Rep. Jernie Jett V. Nisay** (asr-003630)

**[04:05:21.840] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003631)

**[04:05:24.820] Rep. Ma. Alana Samantha Taliño Santos** (asr-003632)  
Mr. Speaker, I move that we recognize Honorable Dada Kiram Ismula of Akbayan Partylist to interpret the budget of the Commission of Higher Education.

**[04:05:36.800] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003633)  
The Honorable Dada Kiram Ismula of Partylist Akbayan is recognized. Please proceed.

**[04:05:44.020] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003634)  
Assalamualaikum, Murahmatullahi Wabarakatuhu.

**[04:05:47.280] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003635)  
Mr. Speaker, Madam Sponsor, noong nakaraang taon sa budget deliberations para sa CHED,

**[04:05:53.680] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003636)  
na itanong natin kung merong datos ang CHED sa dropout rate ng mga IPs.

**[04:06:00.220] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003637)  
Natanggap po natin ang liham ng CHED last year na nagsasabing walang available na datos dito.

**[04:06:07.660] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003638)  
Maliban dito sa IP dropout rates, can I confirm kung may datos na ba ang CHED para sa indigenous people, student, enrollment, retention, and graduation?

**[04:06:23.680] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-003639)  
Sige po.

**[04:06:31.360] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-003640)  
Taking off from your question last year, kung kaya't inumpisahan po ng CHED,

**[04:06:38.600] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-003641)
