**[04:06:44.100] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-003642)  
Pero ang meron po tayo that we can inform our good colleague, yung pong number of scholars.

**[04:06:51.420] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-003643)  
Yes.

**[04:06:53.680] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-003644)  
For 2025, may 226.

**[04:07:06.080] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-003645)  
And for 2026, we have 205.

**[04:07:11.500] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003646)  
Mr. Speaker, Madam Sponsor, naitanong ko po ito dahil gusto ko lang maging consistent, no?

**[04:07:17.320] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003647)  
At gusto ko lang din makita na talagang tinatrabaho na ito ng CHED na i-integrate.

**[04:07:23.680] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003648)  
Or isama na rin talaga sa datos ng CHED yung patungkol sa mga IP datas.

**[04:07:30.580] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003649)  
At dahil wala pa sa ngayon, kaugnay ng aking naunang katanungan sa liham din ng CHED,

**[04:07:38.240] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003650)  
they committed to issue a memorandum that will require HEIs to submit IP student profile.

**[04:07:46.460] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003651)  
Mr. Speaker, Madam Sponsor, maari ko po bang i-confirm ma ang commitment na ito if this has been complied with?

**[04:07:53.680] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003652)  
Dahil ang opisina ko po ay hindi pa rin naka-receive, kaugnay dito sa memorandum na ito.

**[04:08:00.300] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-003653)  
Yes.

**[04:08:00.580] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003654)  
At kung ating pong babalikan yung last year, mismo dito rin po sa plenary, kinomit ng Secretary of CHED yung memorandum na iyon.

**[04:08:11.640] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-003655)  
Yes.

**[04:08:13.460] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-003656)  
As an answer to that, taking off from last year's inquiry, we have, the CHED has a summary total enrollment and graduates per region.

**[04:08:22.400] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-003657)  
So meron po tayong dato.

**[04:08:23.680] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-003658)  
At willing po ang CHED na i-furnish po ang opisina nyo ng kopya na ito.

**[04:08:29.100] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003659)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[04:08:30.200] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003660)  
At kung maaari, i-follow up ko na rin po yung memorandum na iyon.

**[04:08:34.800] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003661)  
Dahil ito'y binanggit po mismo ng Secretary during the budget briefing nung nakaraang taon na pagkatapos na pagkatapos ng budget briefing,

**[04:08:45.280] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003662)  
ay i-release niya ang memorandum na iyon.

**[04:08:53.680] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003663)  
At kung sakaling hindi pa po nakapag-release ng memorandum, itatanong ko na lang po,

**[04:09:00.940] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003664)

**[04:09:05.160] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003665)  
At paano nila masisiguro na ito ay susundin ng ating mga HEIs?

**[04:09:12.440] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-003666)  
Yes.

**[04:09:13.360] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-003667)  
Kaya po nakuha yung datos ay dahil naglabas ng memo.

**[04:09:16.440] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-003668)  
But at any rate, ang karagdaga na ginawa po dyan is under the student records managerial,

**[04:09:23.680] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-003669)  
management system, may IP profiling. Kasama na po siya.

**[04:09:28.640] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003670)  
Maraming salamat, Madam Sponsor.

**[04:09:32.020] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003671)  
Kaya ko po naitanong ang tungkol sa datos na ito para masiguro na natutugunan ang mga concerns ng ating mga HEIs.

**[04:09:39.960] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003672)  
Ngayong hindi pa kompleto ang mga datos o kakasimula pa lang at continuous pa ito,

**[04:09:47.880] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003673)

**[04:09:52.360] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003674)  
ay talagang nakakarating at nakakatulong sa mga IP students na nangangailangan?

**[04:10:04.280] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-003675)  
Sige po.

**[04:10:06.600] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-003676)  
The CHED will enter into a MOA with the LGUs to be able to help them monitor.

**[04:10:15.560] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-003677)

**[04:10:19.680] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-003678)  
they are sure to receive all the...

**[04:10:22.360] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-003679)  
All the benefits that they should from the CHED.

**[04:10:24.800] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003680)  
Maraming salamat, Madam Sponsor.

**[04:10:27.240] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003681)  
Humihingi na lamang po ako ng kopya ng memorandum na ito.

**[04:10:31.100] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003682)  
At kung maaari po, hingi na rin po ako ng datos na available mula sa CHED sa datos ng patungkol sa IP.

**[04:10:40.260] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-003683)  
Sige po.

**[04:10:41.920] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-003684)  
Across all regions, merong 21,953 IPs na nakikinabang.

**[04:10:49.300] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003685)  
Maraming salamat.

**[04:10:49.980] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-003686)  
We will share the same data with you.

**[04:10:52.360] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003687)  
Yes. Maraming salamat po.

**[04:10:53.480] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003688)  
On the matter of indigenous people's studies,

**[04:10:55.760] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003689)  
we note CHED Memorandum Order No. 2, Series of 2019,

**[04:11:02.500] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003690)

**[04:11:08.140] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003691)

**[04:11:18.440] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-003692)

Maraming salamat.

**[04:11:30.700] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003695)

**[04:11:59.600] Rep. Monique Yazmin Maria Q. Lagdameo** (asr-003700)

**[04:12:03.300] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003701)  
Maraming salamat, Madam Sponsor.

**[04:12:05.800] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003702)

**[04:12:10.580] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003704)

**[04:12:15.440] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-003705)

**[04:21:31.570] Rep. Rhea Mae A. Gullas** (asr-003741-split-2)  
Mr. Speaker, I move that we recognize the Honorable Karl Fernandez Legazpi, Vice Chairperson of the Committee on Appropriations, to sponsor the proposed budget of the state universities and colleges.

**[04:21:45.480] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-003741-split-3)  
The Honorable Vice Chair Karl Fernandez Legazpi of Partylist Pinoy Workers is recognized. Please proceed.

**[04:21:53.240] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-003741-split-4)  
Thank you, Mr. Speaker. Mr. Speaker, distinguished colleagues, I rise today to sponsor the proposed fiscal year 2027 budgets of our state universities and colleges.

**[04:22:00.000] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-003742)  
Across the country, including the University of the Philippines system. At the outset, Mr. Speaker, I acknowledge my fellow sponsors who will help defend SUC budgets in their respective areas: Representative Arthur Celeste for Luzon, Representative Emer Lollipop [unclear] and Representative Karl Nicolas [unclear] for the Visayas.

**[04:22:30.000] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-003743)  
And Representative Bernadette Barbers and Representative [unclear] for SUCs in Mindanao. I thank our co-sponsors for taking on this responsibility and helping ensure the concerns and requirements of our state universities and colleges across the country are represented before this House. It is a particular honor for me to stand here on behalf of my own alma mater, the University of the Philippines.

**[04:23:00.000] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-003744)  
But today we speak not only for one university or one region. Together we speak for the students of our state universities and colleges from Luzon, Visayas, and Mindanao, young Filipinos who look to public higher education as their pathway to a better life. Para sa maraming pamilyang Pilipino, ang makapasok sa isang pampublikong unibersidad ay higit pa sa pagtanggap ng diploma. Ito ay pagkakataon na makapagtapos para sa kanilang kinabukasan at sa kinabukasan ng kanilang mga pamilya.

**[04:23:30.000] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-003745)  
Sa bawat pintuang binubuksan ng ating mga SUCs, may pamilyang nabibigyan ng pagkakataong mangarap nang mas malaki. Iyan ang pangakong dala ng ating mga SUCs. Under the proposed fiscal year 2027 national budget, our state universities and colleges are allocated 141.09 billion, an increase of 6.1 billion or 4.5% compared with the fiscal year 2026 National Expenditure Program.

**[04:24:00.000] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-003746)  
Funding for the Universal Access to Quality Tertiary Education program is likewise being increased as more Filipino students enter our public universities. But our responsibility does not end with opening the doors of our universities. Access must come with quality. As enrollment grows, our SUCs must have enough teachers, classrooms, laboratories, equipment, and learning resources.

**[04:24:30.000] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-003747)  
Our campuses

for our students

hindi na po natin

**[04:59:09.760] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004415)  
ng budget briefing at

**[04:59:13.620] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004417)  
ng mga state universities and colleges

na kailangang dagdagan

lalo pa at may mga

nakita tayong significant

**[04:59:23.620] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004422)  
na budget cuts

**[04:59:25.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004423)  
sa state universities and colleges.

**[04:59:27.760] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004424)  
Unang kailangang

**[04:59:29.300] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004425)  
mailatag dito, yung tungkol

**[04:59:31.660] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004426)  
sa free higher education deficiency

**[04:59:33.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004427)  
for 2026

**[04:59:35.980] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004428)  
and 2027.

**[04:59:37.500] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004429)  
Ulitin lang po natin.

**[04:59:40.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004430)  
Malaking tagumpay

**[04:59:41.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004431)  
during the

**[04:59:43.900] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004432)  
last

**[04:59:44.920] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004433)  
year's budget deliberation

**[04:59:47.320] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004434)  
na ipanalo at pinasok ng

**[04:59:49.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004435)  
kongresong ito yung dagdag na

**[04:59:51.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004436)  
12 billion para mabayaran na

**[04:59:53.720] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004437)  
yung

**[04:59:54.960] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004438)  
free higher education deficiency

**[04:59:57.100] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004439)  
from 2022 to 2025.

**[05:00:00.000] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004440)  
Ito yung utang ng gobyerno sa mga state universities and colleges na nagpapatupad ayon sa mandato ng batas ng libreng tuition, libreng edukasyon sa kolehyo.

**[05:00:15.140] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004441)  
So dapat binibigyan ng subsidy ang mga ito pero kulang may utang. For 2026 may utang na naman at may projected na rin for 2027.

**[05:00:30.060] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004442)  
So magkano po ang kailangan for 2026 and 2027 para matiyak ngayon pa lang na wala ng deficiency at hindi na naman magkakaroon ng tumatakbong utang.

**[05:00:45.140] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004443)  
Sa mga state universities and colleges na nagpapatupad ng mandato ng batas na libreng edukasyon sa kolehyo sa ating mga state universities and colleges.

**[05:00:57.920] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004444)  
So magkano po 2026 and 2027?

**[05:01:01.200] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004445)  
Madam Speaker, our senior school league, base po sa datos po na binigay sa atin, ang funding deficiency po for 2026 and 2027 is in the amount of 5.321 billion pesos.

**[05:01:15.140] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004446)  
Pwede pong i-breakdown natin?

**[05:01:17.880] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004447)  
Sa taon po ng 2026, ito po ay sa halaga ng 2.048 billion pesos. Sa taon po ng 2027, ito po ay nasa halaga ng 2.766 billion pesos po, Madam Speaker.

**[05:01:35.460] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004448)  
Okay, thank you for that. So malinaw po, projected na ng mga state universities and colleges na for 2026, meron na namang utang.

**[05:01:45.140] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004449)  
Kapos, ang gobyerno nagpapatupad ng free higher education, yung state universities and colleges, pero kulang ng 2.048 billion.

**[05:01:55.980] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004450)  
Tapos projected na rin po nila for 2027, naabot naman ito sa 2.766 billion, total 5.32 billion.

**[05:02:04.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004451)  
So ngayon pa lang, Madam Speaker, at tanong po natin, sinabit po ba ng pasok?

**[05:02:15.140] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004452)  
At saka ng CHED, ang request for institutional amendments para mapunuhan ito?

**[05:02:22.300] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004453)  
Yes po, Madam Speaker, doon si Skolig, yan po kanilang, ano po.

**[05:02:26.820] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004454)  
Pinabot ito sa Committee on Appropriations?

**[05:02:29.760] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004455)  
Yan po, kanilang dinulog po sa Committee on Appropriations.

**[05:02:32.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004456)  
Okay, so malinaw po, and Madam Speaker, nire-rehistro natin ang buong pagsuporta sa pagpondo ng 5.32 billion pesos.

**[05:02:44.260] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004457)  
Okay.

**[05:02:45.140] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004458)  
At sa PAYAC, na walang deficiency, sapat ang, at least walang utang, ang gobyerno sa pagpondo ng free higher education.

**[05:02:53.900] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004459)  
Pangalawa, kaugnay sa mga bawa sa MOUE.

**[05:03:00.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004460)  
Kapansin-pansin po, ilan po sa proposed budget, ilan ang mga state universities and colleges, 113 in total, ilan ang may budget cut, particular sa MOUE?

**[05:03:15.140] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004461)  
Madam Speaker, doon si Skolig, nasa 58 SUCs po, nabawasan ang kanilang, ano po, MOUE.

**[05:03:23.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004462)  
58 of 113 state universities, may bawa sa MOUE.

**[05:03:31.280] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004463)  
No, so kulang na nga ang MOUE, binawasan pa.

**[05:03:38.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004464)  
Hindi lang binawasan, alam natin na pambihira ngayon ang pagtaas ng inflation rate.

**[05:03:43.980] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004465)  
So lalo pang mas malakas.

**[05:03:45.140] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004466)  
Kaya ang bawas dito, at least 6% ang inflation rate ngayon.

**[05:03:49.700] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004467)  
So, hindi po natin maintindihan kung bakit binawasan.

**[05:03:54.280] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004468)  
Kung sasabihin nilang utilization rate, mababa daw ang utilization rate, sisisihin na naman ng SUCs dito.

**[05:04:01.960] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004469)  
Hindi po katanggap-tanggap yun.

**[05:04:03.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004470)  
Kagabi, interpellation natin ng DILG, yung Local Government Support Fund,

**[05:04:12.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004471)  
yung tinatawag nating...

**[05:04:15.140] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004472)  
LGU Pork ng Presidente, no?

**[05:04:19.960] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004473)  
May git 56 billion ito.

**[05:04:21.900] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004474)  
Ano yung utilization rate nun?

**[05:04:24.640] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004475)  
7%!

**[05:04:26.700] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004476)  
7% utilization rate.

**[05:04:30.540] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004477)  
No, so napakalaki.

**[05:04:31.900] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004478)  
Ngunit, dinadagdagan pa.

**[05:04:33.920] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004479)  
Gusto pa dagdagan.

**[05:04:35.020] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004480)  
So anyway, ang point lang natin,

**[05:04:36.440] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004481)  
there is no justification whatsoever for reducing the MOUE of state universities and colleges.

**[05:04:44.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004482)  
Therefore,

**[05:04:45.140] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004483)  
inilalaban po natin, hindi lang yung restoration ng mga ito, kung di pagtaas pa.

**[05:04:53.460] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004484)  
Meron pong isang proposal,

**[05:04:55.260] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004485)  
ang CHED, na-discussed kanina, yung tinatawag ni CHED Chair Agrupis,

**[05:05:00.620] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004486)  
na leveling up proposal.

**[05:05:03.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004487)  
So, maaari sigurong makabawi ito,

**[05:05:05.280] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004488)  
na ang gagawin daw ay itutumba sa P250 per unit,

**[05:05:11.840] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004489)  
yung minimum na, kumbaga,

**[05:05:15.140] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004490)  
standard tuition fee, or unit, per unit cost ng mga universities.

**[05:05:22.300] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004491)  
And that would mean an additional 4.2 billion pesos for all state universities.

**[05:05:31.060] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004492)  
Sapat na po ba ito?

**[05:05:32.320] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004493)  
Or, acceptable po ba ito sa mga state universities and colleges?

**[05:05:38.080] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004494)  
An additional 4.2 billion pesos, would that be enough to address?

**[05:05:45.140] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004495)  
This shortfall, or this cut, in MOUE.

**[05:05:49.960] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004496)  
Pero banggitin ko lang ha, para may special mention,

**[05:05:53.060] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004497)  
yung UP po, pinakamalaki ang naging cut sa MOUE, 1.9 billion.

**[05:05:57.740] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004498)  
Then, Eastern Stalmar State University, 66 million.

**[05:06:01.500] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004499)  
West Visaya State University, 50 million.

**[05:06:04.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004500)  
Rizal Technological University, 39 million.

**[05:06:08.320] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004501)  
Bicol University, 32 million.

**[05:06:10.360] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004502)  
Yung po yung top 5.

**[05:06:11.260] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004503)  
E ba, magiging ko na rin PUP.

**[05:06:13.140] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004504)  
MOUE, 26.3 million cut.

**[05:06:17.260] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004505)  
Also, I can mention, Don Mariano Marcos Memorial State University,

**[05:06:22.300] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004506)  
yan ang pinanggalingan, former president, yung chair-chair mismo,

**[05:06:26.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004507)  
may cut din po na 20 million sa kanilang MOUE.

**[05:06:29.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004508)  
So, magkano po ang kailangan na dagdag para sa MOUE, if ever?

**[05:07:10.240] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004509)  
Madam Speaker and distinguished colleague,

**[05:07:13.140] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004511)  
Ang nilalais po yung balik ng ating pasok, ating mga university and colleges,

**[05:07:17.640] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004512)  
ay yung 20% lang po na natanggal sa kanilang MOUE budget na nirequest po nila for the 2027 NEP po.

**[05:07:26.960] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004513)  
Okay. So, in other words, masaya na ang SUC sa kung ano yung level noong 2026

**[05:07:35.020] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004514)  
at huwag ibawas yung 20% na cut.

**[05:07:41.820] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004515)  
Yes po.

**[05:07:43.140] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004516)  
So, ito po ang kahilingan nila, Madam Speaker.

**[05:07:45.620] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004517)  
Okay. Let me just note na because of the energy emergency,

**[05:07:51.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004518)  
ay nag-utos ang DepEd na isoli daw ng mga SUC yung 20% ng kanilang MOUE.

**[05:08:01.480] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004519)  
So, parang naging forced savings ito at mukhang ito yung ginamit para ma-augment yung pang-ayuda, diba pa?

**[05:08:07.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004520)  
Actually, nakarating po yan sa atin.

**[05:08:10.920] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004521)  
Sabi ng mga faculty and staff,

**[05:08:13.140] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004522)  
na kinaltasan ng malaki yung MOUE, kaya wala na raw pang kahit nagtitipid sila.

**[05:08:20.220] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004523)  
Alinsunod sa CNA agreement, Collective Negotiation Agreements,

**[05:08:25.820] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004524)  
sinasabi po sa kanila ngayon mukhang walang mapagkukunan ng savings

**[05:08:31.160] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004525)  
that are supposed to be the source of funding for the CNA incentive.

**[05:08:38.220] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004526)  
So, we're bringing that concern here.

**[05:08:42.020] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004527)  
Okay.

**[05:08:42.660] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004528)  
And also, yun na nga.

**[05:08:45.020] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004529)  
Sabi na, sinusuportahan natin ng panawagan na sa minimum, ibalik yung mga cut.

**[05:08:50.900] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004530)  
Hindi bumaba dun sa 2027 level, dapat madagdagan pa.

**[05:08:55.080] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004531)  
How about capital outlay, Mr. Speaker, Madam Speaker, Mr. Sponsor?

**[05:09:01.020] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004532)  
Balita ko, may mga state universities na zero pa nga ang capital outlay.

**[05:09:07.880] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004533)  
Naintindihan natin, sabihin nila, hindi naman pwedeng pareho lang nung

**[05:09:11.540] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004534)  
2026 ang capital outlay. One time yan eh.

**[05:09:17.040] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004535)  
So, pero meron pong narinig tayo na a number of SUCs, zero pa nga ang capital outlay.

**[05:09:23.980] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004536)  
Tama po ba yun?

**[05:09:25.460] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004537)  
Tama po yun, Madam Speaker.

**[05:09:27.720] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004538)  
Ilang state universities ang walang capital outlay?

**[05:09:30.220] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004539)  
Based on the data submitted sa data sa binigay ng PASOK,

**[05:09:34.460] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004540)  
nasa 12 po, 12 ang SUCs na wala pong capital outlay sa kanilang...

**[05:09:40.220] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004541)  
12 SUCs. Pwede po ba natin marinig for the record?

**[05:09:45.880] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004542)  
Sino yung 12 SUCs na walang capital outlay?

**[05:09:56.740] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004543)  
At pinapadagdag din po ng ating PASOK na...

**[05:10:00.600] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004544)  
Sige po.

**[05:10:02.260] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004545)  
Ano po, tumaas pa ang numero, nasa 25 po na SUCs ang walang infrastructure projects.

**[05:10:10.220] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004546)  
So, yung kanina pong 12, ayun po ay ano lang yun, no capital outlay allocation.

**[05:10:16.020] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004547)  
Okay. So, yung absolutely zero?

**[05:10:18.740] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004548)  
Opo, tama po yun, Madam Speaker.

**[05:10:20.080] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004549)  
Ngayon, 25 kung walang infrastructure projects.

**[05:10:24.900] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004550)  
So, maaring may capital outlay pero hindi infrastructure.

**[05:10:27.860] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004551)  
Tama po.

**[05:10:28.220] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004552)  
Yun ang ibig sabihin nun. Okay. Para marinig lang natin, sino yung 12 na yan?

**[05:10:33.320] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004553)  
Para nasa record at para mabantayan natin kung magkakaroon pa rin ba.

**[05:10:38.340] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004554)  
Maari po namin i-furnish po.

**[05:10:40.220] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004555)  
O, sige. Gusto sana natin ilagay sa record. Pero, sige na po. Okay.

**[05:10:46.560] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004556)  
Pahingi na lang, nung listahan ng 12 and the 25 na binabanggit ninyo,

**[05:10:51.260] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004557)  
para ma-follow up din natin, mabantayan.

**[05:10:54.040] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004558)  
No? Okay.

**[05:10:56.100] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004559)  
Next, plantilla items. Plantilla positions.

**[05:11:00.960] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004560)  
Particularly for faculty.

**[05:11:02.400] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004561)  
Alam natin may ongoing na programa para makapag-hire ng...

**[05:11:10.220] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004562)  
administrative or non-teaching positions.

**[05:11:14.160] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004563)  
However, let us take a look at faculty items.

**[05:11:18.120] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004564)  
Ilan po ngayon?

**[05:11:19.460] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004565)  
Dahil po sa free higher education, naturally, lumalaki ang enrollment.

**[05:11:24.560] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004566)  
At naturally, kailangan mo ng mas maraming teachers, faculty.

**[05:11:29.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004567)  
Pero, hindi naman po nagdadagdag ng new items.

**[05:11:33.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004568)  
Therefore, job order and contract of service faculty.

**[05:11:37.560] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004569)  
Ilan na po ngayon?

**[05:11:40.220] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004570)  
Ang mga faculty na job order and contract of service.

**[05:11:49.460] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004571)  
Madam Speaker, Distinguished Colleague,

**[05:11:51.920] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004572)  
nasa 55,000 po ang ating mga job order and contract of service workers

**[05:11:57.600] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004573)  
na kabilang po sa part-time faculty ng ating mga SUCs sa buong Pilipinas.

**[05:12:03.300] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004574)  
So, 55,000 job order. Lahat yan, faculty. Tama po. Is that correct?

**[05:12:10.220] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004575)  
Sama na po yung teaching and non-teaching items po dito.

**[05:12:13.240] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004576)  
Teaching and non-teaching. Well, pwede po bang mabigyan tayo?

**[05:12:16.700] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004577)  
Mukhang wala kayong datos. Ilan dito yung teaching, ilan yung non-teaching?

**[05:12:26.420] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004578)  
Sa lukuyin po, wala pong current breakdown.

**[05:12:29.080] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004579)  
Pero, ano po, they will...

**[05:12:33.440] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004580)  
Ang informasyon po na binigyan ng PASOK, 26,000 po ang teaching positions.

**[05:12:40.220] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004581)  
Out of the 55?

**[05:12:42.640] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004582)  
Na ano po, na job order.

**[05:12:45.020] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004583)  
Okay, 26,000. Ayan po, malinaw na malinaw ah.

**[05:12:49.540] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004584)  
26,000 faculty or teaching positions na job order or contract of service.

**[05:12:59.140] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004585)  
Obviously, malaking injustice ito.

**[05:13:02.800] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004586)  
So, tutulak din po natin na mabigyan ng bagong items,

**[05:13:09.280] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004587)  
ang mga state universities and colleges,

**[05:13:12.500] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004588)  
katulad nung naipanalo natin noong 2016,

**[05:13:15.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004589)  
where 9,000 items were created and distributed to the state universities and colleges.

**[05:13:22.500] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004590)  
Kahit pa pano, at the time, that was one-third of the requirement.

**[05:13:28.400] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004591)  
So, kahit pa pano, nakatulong. Hopefully, magawa uli natin ngayong taon.

**[05:13:35.300] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004592)  
Practically a decade after that.

**[05:13:37.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004593)  
No?

**[05:13:38.740] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004594)  
Okay.

**[05:13:39.280] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004595)  
So, malinaw po ito.

**[05:13:41.060] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004596)  
Last point.

**[05:13:43.120] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004597)  
Last point.

**[05:13:44.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004598)  
I actually already made this point during the interpolation of CHED, the CHED budget.

**[05:13:50.320] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004599)  
However, gusto po natin marinig ito ng ating mga state universities and colleges,

**[05:13:54.560] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004600)  
and for the record, in plenary as well,

**[05:13:57.640] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004601)  
yung babalikan ko yung advokasya natin ngayon, no?

**[05:14:02.020] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004602)  
Ng injustice to instructor one position.

**[05:14:07.580] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004603)  
Yung mga instructor one.

**[05:14:09.280] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004604)  
Yung mga instructor one position natin sa state universities and colleges.

**[05:14:12.220] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004605)  
Stricto po ang requirement.

**[05:14:14.300] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004606)  
Magiging instructor one ka lang with plantilla item kung ikaw ay merong MA or MS.

**[05:14:23.360] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004607)  
No?

**[05:14:25.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004608)  
Subalit, sa SUCs, ang merong MA or MS na instructor one, salary grade 12 lang po.

**[05:14:34.240] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004609)  
Salary grade 12.

**[05:14:36.320] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004610)  
No?

**[05:14:37.400] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004611)  
Samantala, sa ibang mga ahensya ng gubernator,

**[05:14:39.120] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004612)  
sa ibang mga ahensya ng gobyerno, notably sa DEPED,

**[05:14:41.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004613)  
guidance counselor, MA requirement, salary grade 16.

**[05:14:45.540] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004614)  
Master teacher one, MA requirement, salary grade 18.

**[05:14:50.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004615)  
Alam ko sa UP, nakikita ko si Chancellor Vistan eh.

**[05:14:54.020] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004616)  
Yung mga MA holder nila, assistant professor yan.

**[05:14:58.140] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004617)  
Salary grade 18, tama.

**[05:15:00.160] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004618)  
Salary grade 18, assistant professor ang title pag may MA.

**[05:15:06.520] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004619)  
Pero sa mga ibang SUCs, sa ating 112 other SUCs ay salary grade 12.

**[05:15:17.360] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004620)  
So panahon na po para iwas to, itama ang napakatinding injusticia na to.

**[05:15:25.740] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004621)  
So bakit, katulad ng nabanggit ko kanina, kung ikaw ay nurse,

**[05:15:33.120] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004622)  
wala kang MA, registered nurse, mag-apply ka sa government hospital, salary grade 15 ka.

**[05:15:44.020] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004623)  
Pero pag mag-apply ka sa SUC, sa College of Nursing, para magturo, kailangan may MA ka.

**[05:15:50.260] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004624)  
Meron ka ng MA, tinanggap ka, instructor 1, salary grade 12 ka.

**[05:15:55.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004625)  
Bakit po?

**[05:15:55.740] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004626)  
So maling-mali, panahon na para iwas to, may commitment ang CHED.

**[05:16:03.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004627)  
Dahil nilatag na po natin ang problema, hinarap na natin sa Kongreso at sa mga ahensya na concerned.

**[05:16:12.560] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004628)  
CHED, Civil Service Commission, DBM at Kongreso, kailangan iwas to na po natin ang injusticia na to.

**[05:16:20.300] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004629)  
So, nag-commit ang CHED na kagyat.

**[05:16:25.740] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004630)  
Harapin nila ang DBM at Civil Service Commission.

**[05:16:28.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004631)  
So we expect that this will be acted upon soon.

**[05:16:34.360] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004632)  
Ibig po sabihin yan, pag tinaas natin yung instructor 1 to salary grade 16,

**[05:16:41.280] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004633)  
eh dapat po, pati yung ibang mga posisyon, necessarily, kailangan tumaas din.

**[05:16:46.460] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004634)  
Na dapat lamang.

**[05:16:49.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004635)  
Dahil deserve ng mga faculty ang mas mataas na posisyon.

**[05:16:55.740] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004636)  
At po, ay biktima ng inyustisyang ito, dapat harapin na natin.

**[05:17:04.460] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004637)  
Ibigay natin sa ating mga faculty ang deserve nila.

**[05:17:11.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004638)  
Pinagtrabahuhan nyo ang inyong professional qualifications, dapat sweldohan kayo ng tama.

**[05:17:17.440] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004639)  
So, sinusuportan po ba ng sponsor?

**[05:17:24.080] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004640)  
Ang ah...

**[05:17:25.740] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004641)  
Pagwasto natin sa salary grade ng mga instructor 1?

**[05:17:29.600] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004642)  
Yes po, ah, Madam Speaker.

**[05:17:31.720] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004643)  
Ah, kasama nyo po kami sa pagsusulong po ng pagpapataas po ng kanilang salary grade.

**[05:17:37.400] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004644)  
At, ah, bilang pong anak ng isang teacher, ah, ako rin po ay, ah, ano po, sangayon po sa inyong pinapanukala.

**[05:17:43.500] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004645)  
Okay. Maraming salamat po, Madam Speaker.

**[05:17:46.160] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004646)  
Hanggang doon na lamang po ang aking interpellation.

**[05:17:49.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-004647)  
Ah, thank you.

**[05:17:55.740] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-004648)  
Majority Leader.

**[05:17:57.820] Rep. Rhea Mae A. Gullas** (asr-004649)  
Madam Speaker, I move that you recognize the Honorable Reneco, I mean, Sara Elago, for her interpellation.

**[05:18:05.020] Deputy Speaker Janette L. Garin** (asr-004650)  
The Honorable Sara Elago of, ah, Gabriela Partilis is hereby recognized for her interpellation. Please proceed.

**[05:18:15.920] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004651)  
Maraming salamat, Madam Speaker.

**[05:18:18.860] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004652)  
Ang representasyon po na ito mula sa Gabriela Women's Party ay sumusuporta din sa panawagan na dapat pondohan.

**[05:18:25.740] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004653)  
Ng kongresong ito, ang pagtitiyak na mapupunan ang deficiency sa pondo para sa free higher education na nasa 5.3 billion pesos.

**[05:18:38.720] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004654)  
At pangalawa, yung pagre-restore ng pondo ng mga state universities and colleges, lalong-lalong na po sa maintenance and other operating expenses,

**[05:18:48.940] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004655)  
napakahalaga po niyan para sa pagmementina at pagtitiyak na tuloy-tuloy na maayos ang operation ng activities,

**[05:18:55.740] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004656)  
ating mga SUCs. Pangatlo, ang nais ko na lang pong amhingin

**[05:19:00.200] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004657)  
mula sa ating SUCs ay report na may kinalaman sa kanilang

**[05:19:04.900] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004658)  
compliance sa gender and development mandate. Nakita po

**[05:19:09.740] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004659)  
kasi natin mula sa ulat ng CHED na nasa 72% lang ng mga

**[05:19:15.220] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004660)  
state universities and colleges natin ang nagko-comply sa

**[05:19:19.740] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004661)  
pangangailangan at nararapat na pagkakaroon ng Committee on

**[05:19:24.860] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004662)  
Decorum and Investigation na fully funksyonal. Gayun din

**[05:19:29.200] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004663)  
ang pagtitiyak na may mga offices na dudulog at didinig ng

**[05:19:35.540] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004664)  
mga kaso na may kinalaman sa paglabag sa Anti-Sexual

**[05:19:39.880] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004665)  
Harassment Act at sa Bawal Bastos Law. Malaki ang concern

**[05:19:43.560] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004666)  
natin dito, Madam Sponsor, dahil matagal na po nabatas yung

**[05:19:48.160] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004667)  
Anti-Sexual Harassment Act mula 1995, nagulat po tayo na meron

**[05:19:54.160] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004668)  
pa po pala tayo.

**[05:19:54.860] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004669)  
Yung mga SUCs na wala pang maayos na CODI na fully funksyonal

**[05:20:00.760] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004670)  
at pangalawa pagdating sa Bawal Bastos Law, dapat sa visa ng

**[05:20:06.860] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004671)  
pagkakaroon din ng tulak ng CHED Memorandum Order para sa

**[05:20:10.340] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004672)  
pagpapatupad ng Bawal Bastos Law ay nakarating na po ito at

**[05:20:14.380] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004673)  
napapatupad sa lahat ng ating state universities and colleges.

**[05:20:18.620] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004674)  
Ayan na lang po ang ating hihingin mula sa ating Distinguished

**[05:20:21.160] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004675)  
Sponsor at sa ating mga state universities and colleges at ang

**[05:20:24.860] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004676)  
natin pong representasyon ay handa rin naman na magbuo ng mga

**[05:20:30.240] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004677)  
espasyo sa pakikipagtulungan. Halimbawa, sa Philippine Commission on

**[05:20:33.740] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004678)  
Women, sila po ay may mandato na magbigay ng mga capacity building,

**[05:20:38.920] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004679)  
institutional development programs para tumulong sa compliance sa

**[05:20:43.640] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004680)  
Magna Carta of Women at lahat po ng mga batas na may kinalaman, hindi lang

**[05:20:48.760] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004681)  
sa kababaihan, kundi po sa pagkamit ng tunay na mainstreaming

**[05:20:54.860] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004682)  
at integration ng gender and development sa paggamit ng at least 5% na pondo

**[05:21:02.300] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004683)  
ng ating mga state universities and colleges. Maasahan po ba natin mula sa ating

**[05:21:07.380] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004684)  
Distinguished Sponsor na titiyakin natin ang submission ng report tungkol sa

**[05:21:12.320] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004685)  
GAD compliance ng lahat ng SUCs?

**[05:21:15.560] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004686)  
Yes, Mr. Speaker, our Distinguished Colleague. Nangako po ang CHED na sila po ay magbigay po ng

**[05:21:24.860] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004687)  
kasama po ang pasok ng impormasyon po ukol sa mga GAD activities po at

**[05:21:31.080] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004688)  
completion na kanilang, GAD compliance po na kanilang ang mga datos po na mayroon sila.

**[05:21:39.140] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004689)  
At yung lingkod din po, patuloy din po ang aming suporta sa ating GAD initiatives

**[05:21:48.780] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004690)  
para po mas malawig po ang compliance ito sa CHED.

**[05:21:54.860] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004691)  
At mga SUCs naman po natin ay batay po sa pag-relay po ng ating pasok president

**[05:22:03.800] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004692)  
ay generally compliant po sa mga GAD activities at nina-nice po nila na ipakita po yung report

**[05:22:09.940] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004693)  
ng lahat po ng kanilang mga SUCs ukol sa usapin po ng GAD.

**[05:22:16.860] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004694)  
Maraming salamat, Mr. Speaker. May nakuha rin po tayong output mula sa PCW.

**[05:22:24.860] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004695)  
Tungkol sa 2024 GAD utilization at nasabi nga po dito na sa state universities and colleges,

**[05:22:32.080] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004696)  
nasa 6% naman yung spending nila para sa GAD.

**[05:22:35.940] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004697)  
Ngunit nung sinilip po natin yung mga specificong aktividad kung saan po ito ginagastos,

**[05:22:42.920] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004698)  
marami pa rin ay halimbawa may kinalaman po sa observance ng 18-day campaign to end violence against women

**[05:22:51.040] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004699)  
mula November 25 hanggang December 12.

**[05:22:54.860] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004700)  
Nandiyan din po ang tungkol sa selebrasyon natin ng National Women's Month

**[05:22:59.980] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004701)  
at nakita po natin na nananatili pa rin na separate from all other major programs ng SUCs ang ating GAD spending.

**[05:23:09.340] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004702)  
Kaya ho, nararapat din na magkaroon tayo ng tuloy-tuloy na capacity building, institutional development,

**[05:23:16.680] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004703)  
yung focus sa organization mismo within the state universities and colleges.

**[05:23:22.180] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004704)  
Dalawang klase po kasi yan na maaari natin.

**[05:23:24.860] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004705)  
Yung isa po ay nakafocus mismo sa organization ng ating mga SUCs at yung isa naman na maaari po na pagkagastusan

**[05:23:36.760] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004706)  
ay nakatoon naman dun sa mismong stakeholders, ang constituency, at maaari din through extension services ng ating mga SUCs.

**[05:23:47.020] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004707)  
Yun po ang nais nating makita, natiyak ng mga activities, integration, hindi separation,

**[05:23:54.860] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004708)  
makakatulong ho ang pinakahuling ulat ng SUCs para dito.

**[05:23:59.220] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004709)  
Ngayon naman, Mr. Speaker, nabanggit din po ng ating kasamahan kanina,

**[05:24:03.980] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004710)  
si Representative Perci Cendaña, yung tungkol po sa insidente sa Batangas State University.

**[05:24:11.320] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004711)  
Maaari po ba kayong magbigay ng update kung may ginawa ng aksyon ang BATSU tungkol dito?

**[05:24:17.680] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004712)  
Nakita ko rin po dun sa pinakahuling ulat ng PCW na ang Batangas State University po,

**[05:24:24.040] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004713)  
ang number one pagdating sa total GAD allocation sa fiscal year 2024.

**[05:24:31.020] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004714)  
Maaari po ba natin matiyak na pagdating dito sa GAD spending,

**[05:24:35.560] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004715)  
ang programa po natin nakatoon din dun sa mga gender-inclusive na mga policies

**[05:24:44.020] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004716)  
at itiyakin po natin na hindi na mauulit ang kahit anong mga insidente na meron pong mga kaso ng diskriminasyon,

**[05:24:54.040] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004717)  
o kaya nakakaramdam po ng hindi pagsali sa mga palisya ang ating mga estudyante.

**[05:25:02.100] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004718)  
Hindi lang ito para sa estudyante ginawang speaker, ginawang sponsor,

**[05:25:05.980] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004719)  
pero para rin sa ating faculty members, sa ating mga non-teaching personnel,

**[05:25:10.160] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004720)  
napakahalaga po na pag may ganitong kaso, hindi natin papayagan na muling mangyari sa ating mga campuses.

**[05:25:17.520] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004721)  
Yes, to our distinguished colleague, ang presidente po ng Batangas State University,

**[05:25:22.840] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004722)  
kaya nangako po.

**[05:25:24.040] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004723)  
Hindi na po mauulit ang mga ganitong insidente at asahan nyo daw po na ang kanilang mga polisya po patungkol sa GAD

**[05:25:32.880] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004724)  
ay patuloy po nila ipapatupad at siguraduhin po na hanggang sa baba po sa implementation level

**[05:25:37.780] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004725)  
ay hindi po magkakaroon ng hindi pagkakaunawa, hindi po malino na pagkakaintindi ang mga nag-implement po nito.

**[05:25:45.800] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004726)  
At base po sa kanilang pinakahuling report ay ongoing na po yung administrative proceedings po patungkol doon.

**[05:25:54.040] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004727)  
Sa mga nabanggit po ng mga security guard po na allegedly sila po ang naging sanhi po ng itong mga gender-based mga violations

**[05:26:06.120] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004728)  
at patuloy po nilang ipapatupad po ang kanilang mga polisya patungkol po sa GAD.

**[05:26:12.060] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004729)  
Mr. Speaker, Mr. Sponsor, nakalagay din sa CHED Memorandum Order na dapat tiyakin ang ating SUCs,

**[05:26:18.080] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004730)  
ng lahat ng ating HEIs at sa ngayon nga po sa ating State University and Colleges,

**[05:26:24.040] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004731)  
merong konsultasyon pagdating sa mga estudyante, faculty, sa ating education stakeholders,

**[05:26:31.800] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004732)  
ang lahat po ng mga polisya na hindi lang gender-sensitive pero nakalagay din po doon disability-sensitive,

**[05:26:38.680] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004733)  
yung mga policies natin towards inclusion, dapat may konsultasyon.

**[05:26:42.860] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004734)  
Maasahan po ba natin ito sa ating mga State Universities and Colleges?

**[05:26:52.700] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004735)  
Ah, ano po, pinapangako po nila

**[05:26:54.040] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004736)  
na yung konsultasyon po na ito, hindi lamang po sa mga estudyante,

**[05:26:58.460] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004737)  
kundi pati po sa mga kanilang magulang at pati na rin po sa mga guro na mga kasama po sa pamantasan.

**[05:27:05.560] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004738)  
Maraming salamat kayo noong Speaker at sa akin din pong kananasan,

**[05:27:09.240] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004739)  
ang halimbawa na integrate po yung GAD mandate sa orientation,

**[05:27:17.340] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004740)  
pag-offer ng mga gender-sensitivity trainings para sa mga org na nagpaparegister,

**[05:27:24.040] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004741)  
sa kanilang mga campuses, sa kanilang mga eskwelahan, sa hiring ng faculty, non-teaching personnel,

**[05:27:31.140] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004742)  
lahat po yan, dapat po sa lahat ng level ng ating pamantasan ay may ganitong tipo ng tuloy-tuloy na training at capacity building.

**[05:27:40.760] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004743)  
At nakikita ko po dito, Philippine Normal University, isa din po sa mga pinakaaktibo na nagtitrain,

**[05:27:47.460] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004744)  
hindi lang po sa ating mga kapwa-SUCs, pero sa iba't-ibang mga sektor,

**[05:27:54.040] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004745)  
maaari silang makaabot para po sa tunay na inklusibo na edukasyon sa lahat ng antas ng ating sistema ng edukasyon.

**[05:28:03.260] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004746)  
Kaya po, ganun din sa University of the Philippines, meron pong Diliman Gender Office,

**[05:28:09.580] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004747)  
merong Office on Anti-Sexual Harassment, ang kanila pong OWASH.

**[05:28:21.260] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004748)  
Yun po yung hangad natin na sana makita din.

**[05:28:24.040] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004749)  
Ang ating SUC.

**[05:28:24.720] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004750)  
Sigurado po ako, marami pang SUCs na may ganun tipo ng mga focal systems,

**[05:28:30.520] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004751)  
ganun tipo ng mga spesipiko na mga centers at offices,

**[05:28:35.500] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004752)  
at hangad natin gano'ng speaker na dapat may pondo ang lahat ng ito.

**[05:28:39.640] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004753)  
Kaya muli, dapat ma-restore yung mga MOOE budget cuts ng ating mga state universities and colleges.

**[05:28:48.640] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004754)  
Gayun din po, dapat pag-aralan ng kapulungan na ito kung paano pa tataasan,

**[05:28:54.040] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004755)  
yung nailaan na capital outlay budget para sa ating mga SUCs.

**[05:28:58.900] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004756)  
May hirapan po tayong magawa yung mandato sa universal access to quality tertiary education

**[05:29:05.640] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004757)  
kung hindi po sasabay na papalawakin din yung capacity ng mga SUCs na tumanggap ng mas maraming mga scholar ng bayan,

**[05:29:15.140] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004758)  
mas maraming mga faculty members na nagiging regular at nagkakaroon ng security of tenure sa kanilang mga trabaho.

**[05:29:23.120] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004759)  
Mahirap po yung gawin.

**[05:29:24.040] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004760)  
Kung tuloy-tuloy ang pagdaltas ng kanilang pondo at kung hindi natin dadagdagan ang kanilang pondo para sa capital outlay at personal services.

**[05:29:32.700] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-004761)  
Maraming salamat gano'ng speaker. Maraming salamat, distinguished sponsor.

**[05:29:37.180] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-004762)  
Thank you, the Honorable Elago, Majority Leader.

**[05:29:40.120] Rep. Rhea Mae A. Gullas** (asr-004763)  
Mr. Speaker, I move that we recognize the Honorable Dada Kiram Ismula for her interpellation.

**[05:29:45.780] Deputy Speaker Yevgeny Vincente B. Emano** (asr-004764)  
The Honorable Dada Kiram Ismula, a portalist, Akbayan is recognized. Please proceed.

**[05:29:54.040] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-004765)  
As-salamu alaykum wa rahmatullahi wa barakatuhu.

**[05:29:58.080] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-004766)  
Mr. Speaker, distinguished sponsor

**[05:30:00.000] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-004767)  
I just have a few general questions addressed to all our SUCs. Una, in accordance with the Higher Education Modernization Act, ano ang compliance rate of our SUCs on the inclusion of students' councils or students' regents in the Board of Regents or Board of Trustees?

**[05:30:22.860] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-004768)  
Meron ba sa mga SUCs natin ang non-compliant pa rin dito?

**[05:30:30.000] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004769)  
Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Colleague, ano po, ang 100% po ang compliance rate na ni-report po ng ating pasok sa ating mga state universities and colleges?

**[05:30:50.020] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-004770)  
Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Sponsor, maaari bang maibigay ng ating mga SUCs ang compliance rate nila sa creation of committees?

**[05:31:00.000] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-004771)  
on Decorum and Investigation, o CODI, to investigate sexual harassment cases in accordance with the Safe Spaces Act. Meron ba na as of now ay non-compliant pa rin dito?

**[05:31:18.500] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004771-split-2)  
Sa ngayon po, sila po ay nag-report na sila po ay generally compliant. Pero sila po ay willing magbigay po ng impormasyon po sa inyong tanggapan

**[05:31:29.980] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004773)  
patungkol po sa mga detalye po ng bawat state universities and colleges po patungkol po sa kanilang compliance po sa inyong hinihingi.

**[05:31:43.500] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-004774)  
Maraming salamat po, Distinguished Sponsor.

**[05:31:46.860] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-004775)  
Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Sponsor, this representation would like to request a compliance report from our SUCs on how they are implementing provisions

**[05:31:57.300] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-004776)  
supporting students with disabilities and priority for poor families under the Universal Access to Quality Tertiary Education Act.

**[05:32:09.140] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-004777)  
Can our SUCs provide this report before the second reading of the GAB?

**[05:32:16.100] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004778)  
Before po on second reading, they will be able to furnish you a copy po. They have the data po and they will be very much willing po to provide you along with the earlier request po.

**[05:32:27.300] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-004779)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[05:32:29.060] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-004780)  
Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Sponsor, can the SUCs also commit to provide a report on the enrollment rate, dropout rate, and performance of students from the indigenous people sector?

**[05:32:41.500] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-004781)  
Thank you, sir.

**[05:32:50.560] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004782)  
Ang CHED po ay may informasyon po patungkol po diyan.

**[05:32:54.860] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004783)  
At sila rin po ay mag-furnish po ng copy.

**[05:32:57.300] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004784)  
Copy sa inyong tanggapan po patungkol sa mga dropout rates po.

**[05:33:01.980] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-004785)  
Tama po, no?

**[05:33:03.320] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-004786)  
Kanina po, during the CHED budget deliberation, naitanong ko rin po ito at inomit din po ng CHED na magbibigay din po sila ng datos na to

**[05:33:13.000] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-004787)  
dahil ongoing pa nga daw po.

**[05:33:15.360] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-004788)  
At last year, nag-commit din ang CHED na magre-release sila ng memorandum sa mga SUCs na patungkol dito.

**[05:33:24.580] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-004789)  
Kaya naman po, naitanong din natin ito.

**[05:33:27.300] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-004790)  
At hihintayin po ng aking opisina ito.

**[05:33:30.840] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-004791)  
Mr. Speaker, good sponsor, how do our SUCs ensure that they are compliant with CHED Memorandum Order No. 2, Series of 2019,

**[05:33:40.320] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-004792)  
which requires the integration of IP studies?

**[05:33:45.060] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-004793)  
Meron bang available na data ang mga SUCs showing compliance to this memorandum?

**[05:33:57.300] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004794)  
Mula po ng tanggap po ng ating mga SUCs and CHED Memorandum No. 2 po patungkol nga po sa mga IPs natin.

**[05:34:10.300] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004795)  
At sila naman po ay nagkaroon ng, sila po ay nabigyan ng payo po na mag-observe po ng compliance po.

**[05:34:23.340] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004796)  
Kasama na po yung mga application po.

**[05:34:25.420] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004797)  
Pag-identify po ng ating mga...

**[05:34:27.300] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004798)  
kasama po ng mga indigenous communities sa kanilang mga application po sa ating mga SUCs.

**[05:34:34.960] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-004799)  
Mr. Speaker, distinguished sponsor, I would look forward to all the data I have requested from our SUCs

**[05:34:42.960] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-004800)  
before we proceed with the second reading of our House Bill No. 10858.

**[05:34:48.860] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004801)  
Sa inyo po yan, Madam Speaker.

**[05:34:51.980] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-004802)  
Mr. Speaker, Mr. Sponsor, regarding naman po sa capital outlays,

**[05:34:57.300] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-004803)  
ng Polytechnic University of the Philippines o PUP,

**[05:35:01.520] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-004804)  
from our consultation with the students of the PUP,

**[05:35:04.960] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-004805)  
2 to 3 years delayed na ang north wing building ng PUP.

**[05:35:11.240] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-004806)  
However, sa COA report, may 106 million pesos unreleased capital outlay for 2025.

**[05:35:20.140] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-004807)  
Can you illustrate paano i-utilize ang north wing building?

**[05:35:57.300] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004809)  
Mr. Speaker, distinguished colleague,

**[05:36:01.800] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004810)  
base po sa update, the PUP north wing project is being implemented by DPWH.

**[05:36:09.100] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004811)  
So the funds were directly given to DPWH by the national government under our General Appropriations Act.

**[05:36:18.100] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004812)  
So the unreleased capital outlay for 2025 is still with the DPWH.

**[05:36:24.220] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004813)  
But the intent po...

**[05:36:27.300] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004814)  
They were able to acquire 164 million funding under the 2026 budget.

**[05:36:40.940] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004815)  
And they were able to bid out the project.

**[05:36:51.400] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004816)  
And the DPWH was able to...

**[05:36:57.300] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004817)  
implement this.

**[05:36:58.740] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004818)  
And as of now po, they still need...

**[05:37:03.780] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004819)  
Ang ano po, there was actually a failure of bidding.

**[05:37:06.960] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004820)  
At hindi po na-implement ang project.

**[05:37:09.940] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004821)  
And kinakailangan pa po nila ng 500 million to complete the 9-story building.

**[05:37:15.320] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004822)  
Ang plano po nila dun sa 9-story building,

**[05:37:18.480] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004823)  
it will have classrooms for classes and students in the main campus po of PUP.

**[05:37:24.300] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-004824)  
Ibig sabihin, ang status po ng...

**[05:37:27.300] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-004825)  
construction ng north wing building ay hindi pa nasisimulan dahil sa failure of bidding.

**[05:37:33.680] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-004826)  
Tama po ba?

**[05:37:34.360] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-004827)  
Tama po. Failure of bidding po.

**[05:37:35.740] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-004828)  
Ano po kaya yung naging rason kung bakit hindi rin na-release yung capital outlay budget para rito?

**[05:37:42.060] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-004829)  
Dahil din po ba sa failure of bidding?

**[05:37:45.280] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-004830)  
Or may iba pang dahilan?

**[05:37:57.300] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004831)  
Hindi po sila, ang administrasyon po ng PUP ay hindi po na bigyang linaw po ng DPWH

**[05:38:04.640] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004832)  
dahil po yung failure of bidding ay nangyari po sa ahensya po ng DPWH.

**[05:38:09.760] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004833)  
Hindi po nakabilang ang PUP po sa implementation po.

**[05:38:14.520] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-004834)  
Sige po. Ito na lang po.

**[05:38:16.380] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-004835)  
Can the PUP commit na makipagpo-operate, makipagpo-ordinate sa DPWH

**[05:38:22.700] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-004836)  
nang sa gayon ay makomplete na po ang construction ng north wing building?

**[05:38:27.300] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004837)  
Patuloy po ang pakipag-ugnayan po ng PUP sa DPWH para po sa pagkompleto po nitong building na ito

**[05:38:38.980] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004838)  
at they will commit po to coordinate with DPWH

**[05:38:45.600] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004839)  
at patuloy po ang pagbigay ng update sa ating sa kongreso patukol po sa paggawa ng building.

**[05:38:54.640] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004840)  
At nais din po nalang ibigay.

**[05:38:57.300] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004841)  
Linaw po, biging ulat na nakipagpulong na daw po sila sa tanggapan po ng Office of the Secretary ng DPWH,

**[05:39:04.760] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-004842)  
Secretary Vince Nison po.

**[05:39:06.520] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-004843)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[05:39:07.860] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-004844)  
Siguro ang kulang na lang po ay i-update po natin ang mga estudyante po natin no.

**[05:39:13.400] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-004845)  
Dahil itong katanungan ay pinaabot po nila during our consultation.

**[05:39:18.320] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-004846)  
Anyway, Mr. Speaker, I would like to use my remaining time on the floor to make an important manifestation.

**[05:39:25.180] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-004847)  
Sa ilalim ng current proposal,

**[05:39:27.300] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-004848)  
na iulat na 43% ng ating state universities and colleges ay humaharap sa budget cuts para sa susunod na taon.

**[05:39:39.360] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-004849)  
Ang paliwanag ng DBM ay mas mataas pa rin ang budget para sa 2027 kung ikukumpara sa NEP to NEP basis.

**[05:39:49.680] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-004850)  
Mr. Speaker, hindi lamang administrators at faculty ang nag-aalala.

**[05:39:55.180] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-004851)  
Ang mismong mga estudyante,

**[05:39:57.300] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-004852)  
at kabataan natin ay malinaw na nagpapahayag ng kanilang concern.

**[05:40:03.380] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-004853)  
Nararamdaman nila na anumang pagbawas o kakulangan sa budget ay direktang tatama sa kanilang access sa quality education,

**[05:40:13.720] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-004854)  
sa kanilang scholarships, at sa kanilang oportunidad na makapagtapos.

**[05:40:18.800] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-004855)  
In this regard, I manifest my full support for increasing the budget of SUCs for 2027.

**[05:40:27.840] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-004856)  
Strengthening our state universities and colleges means strengthening the future of our youth and the nation.

**[05:40:36.400] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-004857)  
With adequate funding, SUCs can continue to expand access to education, sustain research and innovation,

**[05:40:45.480] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-004858)  
and provide the necessary support for faculty and students.

**[05:40:49.240] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-004859)  
With this support for an increase, I urge our SUCs to assure the House of Representatives

**[05:40:55.720] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-004860)  
that they will ensure the participation of students, teachers, and staff in the governance of their universities.

**[05:41:04.960] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-004861)  
I urge our SUCs to assure the House of Representatives that they will ensure our students,

**[05:41:11.660] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-004862)  
no matter what your SOGI-SC, are protected from sexual harassment in the safe spaces of our schools and universities.

**[05:41:20.200] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-004863)  
That administrators and professors who are found liable for sexual violence are removed.

**[05:41:27.960] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-004864)  
That the students, staffs, and teachers are supported when they want to file a case

**[05:41:34.140] Rep. Dadah Kiram Ismula** (asr-004865)  
instead of sweeping the issue under the rug to preserve the reputation of the university.

**[05:42:40.060] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-004880)

**[05:42:48.600] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-004883)

**[05:42:57.380] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-004885)

**[05:43:23.780] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-004890)

**[05:44:07.480] Rep. Audrey Kay T. Zubiri** (asr-004899)

ng DBM

sa mga SUC.

**[05:59:39.520] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005343)  
Ito na nga, halimbawa lang,

**[05:59:43.060] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005345)  
ang DBM

**[05:59:44.700] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005346)  
at CHED

through the sponsor,

**[05:59:48.240] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005348)  
bakit ganito kalaki

**[05:59:49.240] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005349)  
yung gap sa pondo

**[05:59:51.180] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005350)  
pagdating dito sa PUP,

**[05:59:53.700] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005351)  
Mr. Speaker?

**[06:00:00.000] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-005352)  
Sa ating kaibigan, Mr. Speaker, our distinguished colleague,

**[06:00:09.980] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-005353)  
at pati po sa impormasyon na natin nakalap,

**[06:00:12.840] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-005354)  
ang budget po, ang tuition fee po,

**[06:00:14.780] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-005355)  
ang PUP ay nasa P12 per unit.

**[06:00:19.340] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005356)  
So yun po ang dahilan, yun dahil sa sinisingil po na per unit.

**[06:00:23.660] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005357)  
Tama po ba, Mr. Sponsor?

**[06:00:25.320] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-005358)  
Tama po, tama po.

**[06:00:26.020] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005359)  
So yun po ang justification ng DBM din,

**[06:00:29.800] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005360)  
kung bakit ganito kababa.

**[06:00:31.200] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005361)  
Eh, laki ng request, Mr. Speaker, ng PUP.

**[06:00:35.360] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005362)  
Tapos ang ibibigay ng DBM,

**[06:00:38.720] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005363)  
pagkaliit-liit,

**[06:00:43.140] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005364)  
Mr. Speaker, para lang po sa kabatiran

**[06:00:45.380] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005365)  
ng Department of Budget and Management,

**[06:00:49.240] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005366)  
at ito rin ang nararanasan ng mga state universities and colleges,

**[06:00:53.060] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005367)  
Mr. Speaker,

**[06:00:54.520] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005368)  
alam ba ng DBM anong itsura sa PUP?

**[06:00:59.800] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005369)  
Nakapunta na ba mismo yung Department of Budget and Management at CHED sa PUP?

**[06:01:07.900] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005370)  
Alam ba nila gaano kaliit ang mga classroom dyan?

**[06:01:10.500] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005371)  
And if this is happening in PUP, it is also happening elsewhere,

**[06:01:14.440] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005372)  
in other state universities and colleges.

**[06:01:17.040] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005373)  
Just to give you an example, Mr. Speaker,

**[06:01:19.780] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005374)  
kasi hindi alam ng DBM yung pinagagawa niya eh.

**[06:01:24.680] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005375)  
Sa CR po ng PUP, Mr. Speaker,

**[06:01:27.340] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005376)  
ang tabo po na ginagamit doon,

**[06:01:29.680] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005377)  
yung isang galon ng ketchup,

**[06:01:31.380] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005378)  
buntingin mo, yun ang tabo sa PUP sa CR.

**[06:01:35.340] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005379)  
Yung ceiling fan sa PUP, Mr. Speaker,

**[06:01:39.460] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005380)  
yung mismong masin yung umiikot, hindi yung LEC.

**[06:01:45.540] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005381)  
Sa isang classroom,

**[06:01:48.560] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005382)  
pagkakasahin, mahigit apat na po, limampung estudyante,

**[06:01:52.220] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005383)  
pagkaliit-liit ng classroom, Mr. Speaker.

**[06:01:56.240] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005384)  
Tapos lalagakan yung PUP

**[06:01:58.780] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005385)  
ng galon ng ketchup,

**[06:01:59.680] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005386)  
ganito kaliit na budget.

**[06:02:01.400] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005387)  
Again, Mr. Speaker, our point is,

**[06:02:03.300] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005388)  
if this is happening in PUP,

**[06:02:05.240] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005389)  
it is also happening in other state universities and colleges.

**[06:02:09.460] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005390)  
Bakit ganyan ang DBM kung maglagak ng pondo sa mga SUCs natin?

**[06:02:29.680] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-005391)  
Sa ating kaibigang and our distinguished colleague,

**[06:02:35.820] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-005392)  
mula po sa TED,

**[06:02:38.240] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-005393)  
yun nga pong 12 pesos per unit na tuition fee na sinisingil po.

**[06:02:44.820] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-005394)  
Sila po ay may karoon ng formula

**[06:02:47.180] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-005395)  
para may pataas nga po ang kailangang makala po na pondo

**[06:02:54.920] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-005396)  
para po masagot nga po yung mga kakulangan natin sa pondo.

**[06:02:59.680] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-005399)  
At hindi po natin nakukuha mula sa national budget, mula po sa DBM.

**[06:03:03.520] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-005400)  
At sila po ay gumagawa ng paraan para po mas may palaki po

**[06:03:06.320] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-005401)  
yung allocation po para sa PUP.

**[06:03:10.900] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-005402)  
Mula po sa kanila, dito nga po sa NCR, sa ating SUCs,

**[06:03:15.460] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-005403)  
ang PUP po ang pangalawa sa nakakuha ng pinakamalaking budget po

**[06:03:19.260] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-005404)  
pagkatapos ng University of the Philippines.

**[06:03:22.320] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-005405)  
Para po sa kanila, yun po ang kapatunayan

**[06:03:26.220] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-005406)  
na ang PUP po ay hindi naman po

**[06:03:29.220] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-005407)  
pinapawalang, binibigyang halaga naman po

**[06:03:32.600] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-005408)  
ang pagpupondo po at pag-alaga po sa ating pamantasan.

**[06:03:38.940] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-005409)  
At yun naman po kanila, mula sa kanila,

**[06:03:41.580] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-005410)  
ang commitment naman po na mas mapataas po

**[06:03:46.200] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-005411)  
ang pondong ilalaan po para sa PUP.

**[06:03:49.400] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005412)  
Salamat, Mr. Speaker.

**[06:03:50.680] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005413)  
Naintindihan ko po yung binabanggit ng ating kaibigan na sponsor.

**[06:03:54.680] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005414)  
At naintindihan din naman natin na ginagawa ng CHED

**[06:03:57.120] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005415)  
yung kanyang makakaya para matulog.

**[06:03:59.220] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005416)  
Yung mga state universities and colleges.

**[06:04:01.920] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005417)  
Pero again, matagal na pong problema ng mga SUCs ito eh.

**[06:04:07.700] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005418)  
Nagre-request, sangayon doon sa kanilang pangangailangan.

**[06:04:13.640] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005419)  
Tapos pagdating, pag dumaan na sa DBM,

**[06:04:16.260] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005420)  
pagkalayo na sa realidad.

**[06:04:18.620] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005421)  
Kaya nga hinahamon natin yung DBM

**[06:04:20.360] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005422)

**[06:04:22.860] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005423)

**[06:04:29.220] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005424)  
Yan ang problema sa DBM eh.

**[06:04:33.200] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005425)  
Ako na nga mismo po, Mr. Speaker, ang nagsasabi.

**[06:04:36.420] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005426)  
Buhay na patutuo.

**[06:04:38.420] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005427)  
Mula pagka-estudyante ko hanggang graduate,

**[06:04:41.140] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005428)  
ang tabo namin sa CR,

**[06:04:43.640] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005429)  
gupitin mo yung isang galong ketchup,

**[06:04:45.580] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005430)  
yun na yung tabo.

**[06:04:47.640] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005431)  
Tapos yung DBM sasabihin,

**[06:04:49.420] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005432)  
ginagawa naman namin yung aming makakaya.

**[06:04:52.220] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005433)  
Just to prove my point, Mr. Speaker,

**[06:04:55.520] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005434)  
even the secretary of DBM,

**[06:04:58.100] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005435)  
iskolar ng bayan,

**[06:04:59.220] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005436)  
and then,

**[06:05:01.500] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005437)  
taga-UP.

**[06:05:04.440] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005438)  
Ang punto ko, Mr. Speaker, Mr. Sponsor,

**[06:05:07.580] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005439)  
dapat nababatid at naiintindihan ng DBM mismo

**[06:05:10.840] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005440)  
anong kalagayan ng mga state universities and colleges.

**[06:05:15.360] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005441)  
Yun po ang punto dito, 100,000.

**[06:05:17.800] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005442)  
Napipenalize pa yung PUP

**[06:05:19.200] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005443)  
and other state universities and colleges.

**[06:05:22.080] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005444)  
Kasalanan pa nila ngayon.

**[06:05:23.280] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005445)  
Gagawa tuloy ng iba't-ibang paraan

**[06:05:25.200] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005446)

**[06:05:29.220] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005447)  
ng mga SUCs.

**[06:05:30.280] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-005448)  
And I understand.

So there was never a time na ang isang scholar po na multi-year, hindi natin nasagot yung isang taon niya.

**[06:21:09.780] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-005887)  
Hindi natin nabayaran.

**[06:21:12.160] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-005888)  
Nagkaroon po ng delays, aminado po ang Ched yan, na nagkaroon po ng delays sa releases.

**[06:21:16.600] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-005889)  
So balit na-address naman po at sa kasulukuyan po wala naman na po backlogs ng releases.

**[06:21:24.160] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005890)  
Ano po nagiging epekto ng delay para doon sa sudyante?

**[06:21:30.940] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-005891)  
Nag-epekto, Distinguished Colleague, ay yung mga releases na allowances.

**[06:21:36.100] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-005892)  
So balit nakakapasok naman daw po sa kanila mga klase,

**[06:21:39.620] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-005893)  
ang naging epekto po sa mga delays ay yung mga kanilang pagtanggap ng mga allowances.

**[06:21:45.380] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-005894)  
So balit, ito naman po.

**[06:21:46.600] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-005895)  
So nabigyan na po, linaw ng Ched na na-release na po ang mga pending po ng mga allowances natin mga scholars.

**[06:21:56.500] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005896)  
Are we monitoring that? Kung ano po yung epekto?

**[06:21:59.540] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005897)  
Kasi syempre, tayo po, nagbibigay tayo ng scholarship, rinaprioritize natin 4P.

**[06:22:04.840] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005898)  
Diba? Anak ng 4P.

**[06:22:06.900] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005899)  
Ngayon, yung allowance niya na delay, ganong kadagal po yung delay?

**[06:22:16.600] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-005900)  
Ang mga, ang tagal daw po ng delays, ranges from one month to one semester po, ano.

**[06:22:31.140] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-005901)  
Ganong po katagal ng releases.

**[06:22:32.840] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-005902)  
So balit, ang proseso po kasi, from Ched, ay nada-download po ang mga pondo sa SUCs.

**[06:22:39.640] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-005903)  
Ito naman po, nire-report sa kanila para po malaman kung saan nga po yung nagiging saninang delays.

**[06:22:45.780] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-005904)  
At ito po.

**[06:22:46.600] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-005905)  
So, baray sa kanilang pag-uulat, na-address naman na daw po ngayon.

**[06:22:51.540] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-005906)  
At kanila pong, ina-assure na hindi po magkakaroon ng additional delays po sa mga existing scholars.

**[06:22:57.360] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005907)  
So hindi na po mangyayari yan?

**[06:22:59.080] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-005908)  
Yun ang po kanilang commitment, na hindi na po mangyayari ang ganitong delays sa pag-release po.

**[06:23:03.140] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005909)  
Kasi po, isipin nyo kung anak ka ng 4P, diba?

**[06:23:06.720] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005910)

**[06:23:11.140] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005911)  
Ano po ang pakiramdam mo? Saan ka kukuha ng ano?

**[06:23:13.540] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005912)

**[06:23:17.460] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005913)

**[06:23:20.540] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005914)

**[06:23:26.100] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005915)  
Yung mga estudyante na dumadaan po dun sa ganun delay, na nadi-delay po yung allowance nila?

**[06:23:30.880] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005916)  
Thank you.

**[06:23:46.600] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-005917)  
Mga ganitong senaryo po, mahirap nga po para sa mga ating mga estudyante.

**[06:23:50.560] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-005918)  
At narinig naman po nila, mga dinulog po ng mga ganitong feedback na may epekto nga po sa kanilang pag-aaral.

**[06:23:56.800] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-005919)  
At bilang solusyon po dyan, ngayon po, bilang kanilang ginagawang polisiya,

**[06:24:03.080] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-005920)  
may ongoing, sa mga ongoing, ngayong September pa lamang,

**[06:24:07.340] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-005921)  
inire-release na po nila kanilang mga scholarship, kanilang pondo sa mga scholars para po sa mga ongoing na programs.

**[06:24:15.600] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005922)

**[06:24:21.200] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005923)

**[06:24:26.640] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005924)  
Meron naman po sigurong miscellaneous fund ang mga SUCs na baka pwedeng pagkuna ng allowance nila pag nadidelay yung pagbayad natin.

**[06:24:41.740] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005925)  
Pusible po ba yun?

**[06:24:43.980] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-005926)

**[06:24:44.880] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-005927)

**[06:24:49.800] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-005928)

**[06:24:56.660] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-005929)

**[06:25:03.600] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005930)
