**[06:25:13.500] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005931)  
So, ano po?

**[06:25:14.880] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005932)

**[06:25:25.360] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-005933)  
ang huwag naman po mag-antay ay yung mga bata.

**[06:25:44.880] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-005934)

**[06:25:51.320] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-005935)  
ay yung mula po sa umpisa ng semestre,

**[06:25:55.360] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-005936)  
kailangan na po nilang maybigay ang billing statement para po sa mga scholars.

**[06:26:02.480] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-005937)  
Ayun po yung magiging udyok po para mas mabilis po yung processing ng pag-release ng scholarship funds.

**[06:26:08.820] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-005938)

**[06:26:14.880] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-005939)  
magiging best practice po para sa lahat ng mga SUCs para po may patuloy na po yung early billing para po sa ating mga estudyante.

Mr. Speaker, sa mga nakapagtapos at nakapasa na sa licensure exam, ilan ang nagsasagawa na ng return service?

**[06:30:24.780] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-005968)

**[06:30:38.100] Rep. Karl Fernandez Legazpi** (asr-005969)

International

**[06:58:02.240] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006425)

**[06:58:06.340] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006427)

**[06:58:08.560] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006428)  
and we as a government have been

**[06:58:20.580] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006434)

**[06:58:26.900] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006437)

**[06:58:36.440] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006442)

**[06:58:38.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006443)

**[06:58:42.560] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006445)

**[06:58:48.260] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006448)

**[06:58:52.140] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006450)

**[06:58:54.500] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006451)

These circumstances

**[06:58:59.740] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006454)

**[06:59:06.900] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006458)

**[06:59:14.940] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006462)

**[06:59:28.340] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006469)

**[06:59:31.360] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006470)  
and we are fortunate

**[06:59:32.620] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006471)

**[06:59:58.260] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006484)  
and ensured adequate

**[06:59:59.920] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006485)  
supply of energy.

**[07:00:00.000] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006486)  
It also looked inward by proactively issuing policies encouraging judicious use of energy, kept price adjustment honest, and observed the market with a keen eye.

**[07:00:17.340] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006487)  
In particular, the DOE, a high level of fuel inventory with an average supply of 50 days at any given point.

**[07:00:26.780] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006488)  
Its interventions also helped avert electricity price spikes estimated at 7 pesos to 12 pesos per kilowatt hour.

**[07:00:37.620] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006489)  
But energy security is not only about surviving a crisis.

**[07:00:42.800] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006490)  
It is about expanding access and securing the resources we will need in years ahead.

**[07:00:49.080] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006491)  
The DOE has achieved a 95.12 household electrification rate,

**[07:00:55.840] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006492)  
with more than

**[07:00:56.780] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006493)  
29.16 million households energized out of approximately 30.6 million potential households across Luzon, Visayas, and Mindanao.

**[07:01:10.940] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006494)  
It has likewise unlocked new potential from our own energy resources through the discovery of more than 222 billion cubic feet of estimated new gas reserves in Malampaya.

**[07:01:24.920] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006495)  
The sources with the potential to provide power equivalent to the consumption of approximately 13.11 million households for one year.

**[07:01:35.220] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006496)  
The National Electrification Administration has also reached a 91.07 household energization level, covering 1,485 municipalities and cities, 36,072 barangays,

**[07:01:52.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006497)  
and 131,704 sitios

**[07:01:56.080] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006498)  
with more than

**[07:01:57.240] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006500)  
17.327 million connections.

**[07:02:01.520] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006501)  
And as we address the President's call to reduce systems loss charges,

**[07:02:07.100] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006502)  
NEA has formulated an action plan to address both non-technical and technical losses.

**[07:02:16.860] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006503)  
This includes stronger measures against electricity pilferage,

**[07:02:22.560] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006504)  
improved billing collection efficiency,

**[07:02:24.920] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006505)  
better meter management and audits,

**[07:02:28.760] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006506)  
and optimization of power distribution infrastructure.

**[07:02:32.900] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006507)  
Mr. Speaker, these efforts demonstrate that energy security requires more than keeping the light on today.

**[07:02:41.700] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006508)  
It requires expanding access, protecting consumers, reducing inefficiencies,

**[07:02:48.420] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006509)  
and securing reliable sources of energy for tomorrow.

**[07:02:53.060] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006510)  
In this regard,

**[07:02:54.920] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006511)  
I urge Congress to support the DOE's additional budgetary request

**[07:03:01.260] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006512)  
so that it may implement its regulatory enforcement and distribution enhancement

**[07:03:08.160] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006513)  
to reduce systems loss policy, audit generation companies,

**[07:03:13.860] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006514)  
power plants to ensure reliability and efficiency,

**[07:03:17.860] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006515)  
and to reduce power outages,

**[07:03:20.940] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006516)  
modernize cybersecurity and automation measures,

**[07:03:24.920] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006517)  
strengthen petroleum product testing to ensure quality,

**[07:03:29.920] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006518)  
undertake necessary structural repairs,

**[07:03:33.280] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006519)  
establish a standby response fund to restore power,

**[07:03:37.940] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006520)

**[07:03:39.940] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006521)

**[07:03:53.400] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006523)  
for the Province

**[07:03:56.760] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006525)

I likewise urge

We are asking them to protect

**[07:06:46.240] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-006610)

My fellow legislators,

There are 30 million homes

**[08:01:01.580] Rep. Renee Louise M. Co** (asr-008409)

Mr. Speaker, sa ilalim ng Republic Act No. 12316, binigyan ng Pangulo ng kapangyarihang magsuspinde o magbawas ng excise tax sa petroleum products kapag ang average ng Dubai crude oil price ay umabot o lumagpas na sa $80

**[08:05:40.000] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-008460)  
kada bariles. Ang rekomendasyon ay manggagaling sa DBCC in coordination sa DOE. Maaari po ba nating malaman kung ano ba ang naging rekomendasyon ng DOE sa DBCC? Lalong-lalo na po ngayon sunod-sunod ang oil price hikes. Noong September 22 lamang nagpatupad na naman ng panibagong malaking oil price hike.

**[08:06:09.980] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-008461)  
Kada litro sa diesel nasa 6.47, sa kerosene 4.88, sa gasolina [unclear]. Ayon mismo sa DOE, ito na ang ikatlong sunod na malaking oil price increase ngayong Setyembre. **[08:06:39.980] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-008462)

**[08:06:53.320] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008462-split-2)  
Just to clarify, Mr. Speaker, ang trabaho po ng DOE ay only to submit the certification sa DBCC na nag-over na po tayo sa $80 per barrel. But it is up to the DBCC to act on it.

**[08:07:09.980] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008463)  
Wala na po sa kapangyarihan ng DOE.

**[08:07:15.000] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-008463-split-2)  
Mr. Speaker, malinaw po naabot o lumagpas na doon sa trigger na presyo. May certification na rin mula sa DOE. Ang kinakailangan na lang po ay agarang aksyon sa pagpapababa sana po ng presyo ng petroleum products. At kahapon lamang naglabas ng anunsyo ang DOF na sususpendihin po ang excise

**[08:07:39.980] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-008464)

**[08:08:15.200] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008465)  
Yes. As much as we want to answer, Mr. Speaker, wala na po kasi sa mandato ng DOE. It's up to the economic managers to decide. Mahirap pong sumaklaw sa kapangyarihan na hindi nakapaloob sa Department of Energy.

**[08:08:36.000] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-008465-split-2)

**[08:09:09.980] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-008467)  
Nandiyan din ang mga nabanggit kong jeepney drivers, operators, companies na nananawagan ng dagliang aksyon. Ang sinasabi po sa atin, hindi lang ng DOE kundi ng iba pang departamento, DOF ang dapat kalampagin pagdating sa agarang rekomendasyon para sa aksyon ng Malacañang.

**[08:09:39.980] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-008468)

**[08:10:09.980] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-008469)

**[08:19:02.460] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-008513)

Tapos, tumaas ang presyo ng gasolina at diesel, LPG, lahat na.

Ngayon, at the height of that crisis, bumili po ng diesel, direct procurement ng diesel at LPG,

**[08:26:16.320] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008616)

**[08:26:44.080] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008620)  
Ayon po sa inyong datos, yung 21,000 liters of LPG

ay,

**[08:26:53.840] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008622)  
alos, mahigit, kumulang kalahati po nito ay,

**[08:27:23.840] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008630)

**[08:27:30.060] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008631)  
So, ano po nangyari dun sa,

**[08:27:47.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008635)  
LPG players.

**[08:27:48.420] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008636)  
So, ano po ang nangyari dito?

**[08:27:51.400] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008637)  
So,

um,

We bought it at,

ah,

At 50,

bought at 70,

Yes.

**[08:28:40.660] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008686)  
To commercial providers.

**[08:28:42.380] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008687)  
Tama po.

**[08:28:43.080] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008688)  
Yes.

**[08:28:43.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008689)

So, high-profile po itong mga purchases na ito.

**[08:29:38.540] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008718)  
Some of them, if I'm not mistaken,

**[08:29:41.140] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008719)  
were direct purchases from Russia.

**[08:29:46.300] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008720)  
So, umabot daw sa

**[08:29:49.740] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008721)  
200 liters na lang gagamitin natin

**[08:29:52.080] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008722)  
para standard,

**[08:29:53.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008723)  
268,000,

**[08:29:56.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008724)  
more than 268,000 liters

**[08:29:58.640] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008725)  
ang binili.

**[08:30:00.000] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008726)  
ayon sa datos na sinubmit nyo, tapos ngayon, 181.7 thousand liters in storage.

**[08:30:09.460] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008727)  
Still in storage.

**[08:30:11.160] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008728)  
Correct, Mr. Speaker, but I would like to clarify, wala po tayong binili sa Russia.

**[08:30:15.700] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008729)  
Ah, okay. Wala pong binili sa Russia.

**[08:30:18.460] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008730)  
Yes, Mr. Speaker.

**[08:30:18.900] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008731)  
Okay. Thank you for that clarification. So, saan po binili? More or less, ah...

**[08:30:26.200] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008732)  
Traders, ah...

**[08:30:27.140] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008733)  
Traders.

**[08:30:27.500] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008734)  
Mr. Speaker.

**[08:30:28.020] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008735)  
Through Singapore. Parang ganun po.

**[08:30:30.760] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008736)  
Yes, Mr. Speaker.

**[08:30:31.800] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008737)  
Sige, walang binili sa Russia. Okay.

**[08:30:36.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008738)  
So, may 181.7 thousand liters still in storage as of your submission, August.

**[08:30:45.400] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008739)  
So, ganun pa rin po ba? This is still in storage?

**[08:30:47.360] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008740)  
Yes, you are correct, Mr. Speaker.

**[08:30:48.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008741)  
Okay. Of the...

**[08:30:50.880] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008742)  
More or less, Mr. Speaker.

**[08:30:52.060] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008743)  
More or less. And this is diesel and gasoline. Tama po?

**[08:30:57.100] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008744)  
Yes, Mr. Speaker.

**[08:30:58.360] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008745)  
Okay.

**[08:30:58.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008746)  
Okay. Ngayon, tinatanong po natin, anong nangyari doon sa more or less 80... roughly 80 thousand liters. What happened to that?

**[08:31:12.960] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008747)  
Meron po kayong report that 6.4 million liters were sold to PNOCEC for off-grid SPAG power generation at a preferential...

**[08:31:28.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008748)  
...preferential rate of 80 pesos per liter.

**[08:31:30.960] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008749)  
Yes.

**[08:31:31.460] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008750)  
Tama po?

**[08:31:32.160] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008751)  
You are absolutely correct.

**[08:31:33.840] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008752)  
Yes. Yan po yung datos na sinabit nyo. So, in other words, nakabili kayo ng diesel. At that time, mataas pa yung presyo nun. That was over a hundred. Tapos, binenta nyo at a preferential rate. So, mas mura. 80 pesos per liter para dun sa missionary areas na gumagamit ng diesel generators.

**[08:31:52.660] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008753)  
Yes.

**[08:31:53.160] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008754)  
So, presumably, nakatulong sa pagpapababa o pag-maintain.

**[08:31:58.360] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008756)  
Nung mas mababang presyo ng kuryente. Okay. Ano po nangyari doon sa iba pa?

**[08:32:04.660] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008757)  
It was sold to the traders, Mr. Speaker.

**[08:32:08.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008758)  
No, kasi 6.4 million lamang yung binenta sa PNOCEC for off-grid.

**[08:32:19.460] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008759)  
So, meron pang natitira po na more than 80 thousand liters na...

**[08:32:28.360] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008760)  
Unaccounted for in your report.

**[08:32:31.800] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008761)  
Yes. Binenta rin po yan sa mga oil companies. Especially yung mga nakikigamit po tayo ng mga facilities po nila.

**[08:32:39.440] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008762)  
Kasi wala po tayong facility. So, dun po napunta yung pagbenta po.

**[08:32:44.300] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008763)  
So, binenta sa oil companies at a preferential rate of 80 pesos per liter? Is that it?

**[08:32:50.640] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008764)  
No. No.

**[08:32:51.100] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008765)  
No. At o, yun po. Pwedeng magpaliwanag po.

**[08:32:54.240] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008766)  
Actually, there's a formula. Yung pagbenta based on...

**[08:32:58.360] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008767)  
Singapore MOPs price, Mr. Speaker.

**[08:33:02.800] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008768)  
Ah, so based on the prevailing MOPs price.

**[08:33:07.040] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008769)  
Yes. Magkaiba po yung treatment.

**[08:33:09.600] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008770)  
Okay.

**[08:33:10.140] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008771)  
Yung sa NAPOCOR and sa oil companies, magkaiba po yan.

**[08:33:13.200] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008772)  
Okay. So, mula nung pumasok po ito in several shipments, ay binibenta nyo na po sa private companies. Ganun po ba?

**[08:33:25.840] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008773)  
Not necessarily, Mr. Speaker.

**[08:33:28.360] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008774)  
It took a while also.

**[08:33:29.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008775)  
So, what is... Pwedeng paki-explain. What was the policy? How is this... How did the DOE dispose of this... At least a portion of this purchase, direct purchase by government?

**[08:33:44.760] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008776)  
Anong patakaran?

**[08:33:45.660] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008777)  
Naintindihan natin. Sige, pagbenta tayo sa PNOCEC para sa missionary electrification.

**[08:33:52.300] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008778)  
What do you mean?

**[08:33:52.760] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008779)  
Pero, paano po yung sa private companies? What is the policy there?

**[08:33:55.880] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008780)  
Sir, first, ah, Mr. Speaker, may supply po tayo. Okay?

**[08:33:59.360] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008781)  
Okay.

**[08:33:59.620] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008782)  
Regarding the policy on pagbenta po sa private, we follow the Singapore MOPs price.

**[08:34:05.580] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008783)  
Okay.

**[08:34:06.580] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008784)  
Hindi po kasi pwedeng specific kasi iba-iba po yung nangyayari. Like, every week, every day, nag-iba po yan. World market, nag-iba yan.

**[08:34:14.540] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008785)  
So, ang ginawa lang po talaga ng ating government is to make sure na may supply po tayo ng diesel.

**[08:34:22.800] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008786)  
Lalo na kung wala tayong supply ng diesel, mas problema po.

**[08:34:25.880] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008787)  
Para sa ating bayan.

**[08:34:27.040] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008788)  
Pero, para po, paano po nakatiyak ang gobyerno at ang DOE na yung binenta sa private ay nag-translate sa mas mababang presyo?

**[08:34:39.980] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008789)  
Baka mamaya, pinatungan din nila, di lugi ang taong bayan, ang taxpayer at ang consumer.

**[08:34:49.480] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008790)  
Yes. Actually, Mr. Speaker, ang pagbenta po ng DOE o ng PNOC.

**[08:34:55.880] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008791)  
Sa ating mga oil players, depende pa rin sa market price. So, yun din po yung sinusunod natin.

**[08:35:02.800] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008792)  
Okay. So, market price. So, hindi necessarily naka-resulta sa pagbaba ng presyo.

**[08:35:08.980] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008793)  
Yes.

**[08:35:09.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008794)  
Okay.

**[08:35:09.980] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008795)  
Well, hindi po ba pwede? Bakit hindi po ginawa? Alam ko pinag-uusapan nyo noon, bakit hindi ginawa na direct ang ibenta ng DOE at a lower rate yung diesel?

**[08:35:21.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008796)  
And you still have 181,000.

**[08:35:25.880] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008797)  
181,000 liters in storage, as reported.

**[08:35:29.080] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008798)  
We truly understand that, Mr. Speaker. But there are also restrictions within DOE, lalo na po sa COA. So, wala po sa mandato ng DOE na magbenta po ng…

**[08:35:40.280] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008799)  
So, sinasabi nyo po, nakatali ang kamay ng DOE, hindi pwede yung direct… some arrangement where this diesel and gasoline may be sold at a preferential rate, at a lower rate.

**[08:35:55.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008800)  
To the consumers. So, yung po sinasabi nyo. Or, di kaya, through the… yung fuel subsidy program, hindi po ba pwede yun?

**[08:36:06.440] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008801)  
Actually, fuel subsidy, we can do it. But it's not really the DOE's doing it.

**[08:36:11.540] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008802)  
Okay.

**[08:36:12.420] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008803)  
Implementing it, Mr. Speaker.

**[08:36:13.980] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008804)  
So, ano po ang plano?

**[08:36:15.060] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008805)  
The DOTr po yung nag-implement po noon.

**[08:36:17.320] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008806)  
Ano po ang plano ng DOE sa 181 million liters in storage?

**[08:36:25.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008807)  
Diesel and gasoline. Ganun din lang po. Benta pa rin sa pribadong sektor.

**[08:36:32.220] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008808)  
Yes. Yung 181, we have to dispose it in a calibrated way, Mr. Speaker.

**[08:36:37.800] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008809)  
We have to be very prudent. We have to study it. Hindi po basta ang ididispose mo.

**[08:36:42.340] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008810)  
We have to study also the situation, not only in the Philippines, but the world market.

**[08:36:49.080] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008811)  
Pero ang masiguro lang po natin na hindi po maubusan ng supply ng diesel ang Pilipinas.

**[08:36:54.440] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008812)  
Well, for sure.

**[08:36:55.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008813)  
May lugi na po dyan. Dahil ito, binili ito, nasa mga 120, 150 pesos per liter ang diesel at the time.

**[08:37:04.340] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008814)  
E ngayon, hindi pa naman. Umaabot. Pero hindi pa lumalag pa sa, I think, 100 pesos per liter ang diesel.

**[08:37:15.240] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008815)  
So, definitely, kung ibenta mo ngayon ito, definitely may lugi.

**[08:37:18.720] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008816)  
Kaya nga po, dapat maingat na we don't, hindi natin gustong i-subsidize yung tubo ng,

**[08:37:25.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008817)  
ng, ah, gobyerno or malugi ang gobyerno unnecessarily. Diba?

**[08:37:30.540] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008818)  
Yes, you are absolutely correct, Mr. Speaker.

**[08:37:33.040] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008819)  
Okay. Anyway, so, binabantayan po natin ang mangyayari dito.

**[08:37:37.740] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008820)  
Ah, ngayon, since we're on the topic, ah, ano po, I mean, at ayon sa mga, ah, analyst, ay mukhang tatagal pa yung crisis sa supply

**[08:37:53.380] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008821)  
ng, ah, fuel.

**[08:37:55.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008822)  
With, ah, still the problem in the Strait of Hormuz. Diba?

**[08:38:01.040] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008823)  
Yes.

**[08:38:01.800] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008824)  
So, tumataas na naman muli ang presyo, lalo na, specifically of diesel, not just because of the supply, but issues with refining capacity globally.

**[08:38:12.060] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008825)  
No? Ah, it's also the war in Iraq, ah, sorry, the war in Ukraine. Diba? The Ukraine war, ah, which has resulted in destruction of, ah, refining capacity.

**[08:38:25.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008827)  
In, ah, Russia, among others. Yung Iran war naman, ah, may epekto sa refining capacity, obviously, in the Gulf states. And Iran itself. Okay?

**[08:38:38.400] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008828)  
Ah, so, mukhang tatagal pa to. Ah, ano po ang kakayahan ngayon ng, ah, DOE at ng gobyerno para magpatuloy sa mga, ah, safety net or mitigating measures?

**[08:38:51.900] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008829)  
May sapat na pondo pa po ba?

**[08:38:55.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008830)  
Ang DOE, ah, o ang administrasyon in general, alam ko bahagi naman ng DOE sa pagpaplano dyan, ah, para matugunan.

**[08:39:05.640] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008831)  
Paano kung pumalo muli tayo sa more than 100 pesos per, per liter na diesel na hindi malayong mangyari yan? No? In the near future.

**[08:39:15.440] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008832)  
I agree, Mr. Speaker, but, ah, ang DOE po is policymaking body, but at the end of the day, executive decision po yan. What we can do is make recommendations.

**[08:39:25.180] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008833)  
We can make studies. Ah, we can recommend to the DBCC, but at the end of the day, ah, economic managers po ang mag-de-decide.

**[08:39:34.220] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008834)  
Okay. Now, as to the storage, so, meron kayong, ah, 181.7 liters in storage in several facilities. Ah, tama po ba isa sa mga pinakamalaking facilities for storage is the one in Subic? Is that correct?

**[08:39:50.760] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008835)  
Yes, Mr. Speaker.

**[08:39:51.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008836)  
Oo. At, ah, yung...

**[08:39:55.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008837)  
Yung facility na yun sa Subic is that the Philippine Coastal Storage, ah, facility in Subic. That is the single largest fuel storage facility in the country, correct?

**[08:40:07.380] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008838)  
Yes, Mr. Speaker.

**[08:40:08.720] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008839)  
At ginagamit po ba ito ng DOE para sa kanilang, ayun, yung binanggit natin?

**[08:40:14.120] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008840)  
Yes, Mr. Speaker.

**[08:40:15.220] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008841)  
How much of the 181 million liters is there?

**[08:40:25.180] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008842)  
Ah, in barrels, we have, ah, about 900, 8,000 to 900,000 barrels, ah, Mr. Speaker.

**[08:40:34.520] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008843)  
Ah, in there?

**[08:40:35.900] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008844)  
Yes, Mr. Speaker.

**[08:40:36.860] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008845)  
Okay. So, hindi lang yung, ah, yung, yung purchase kung hindi more than that? Parang ganun po ba? Okay. Ah...

**[08:40:46.020] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008846)  
Um, barrels po, ah, Mr. Speaker.

**[08:40:48.940] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008847)  
Barrels.

**[08:40:50.320] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008848)  
Barrels. Barrels.

**[08:40:51.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008849)  
159 liters per barrel.

**[08:40:52.700] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008850)  
159 liters per barrel.

**[08:40:55.020] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008851)  
Okay.

**[08:40:55.040] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008852)  
So, if you compute it...

**[08:40:55.520] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008853)  
Ah, 159 million liters. Is that correct?

**[08:40:59.020] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008854)  
No, 159 per barrel. So, compute ka nga. More or less, Mr. Speaker.

**[08:41:03.680] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008855)  
Ah, barrel. Barrel. Oh, you're using barrels. Oh, I get it. Okay.

**[08:41:06.900] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008856)  
Yes, barrels, yeah.

**[08:41:07.420] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008857)  
O, sige po. Anyway, so ginagamit. Pero ang isang issue po dito, of course, this is privately owned.

**[08:41:13.520] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008858)  
And currently, the majority owner of that is, in fact, the U.S. investment firm, I-Squared.

**[08:41:25.040] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008859)  
Tama po ba?

**[08:41:26.620] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008860)  
Yes, Mr. Speaker.

**[08:41:27.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008861)  
Okay. Wala po ba kayong nakikitang issues that a significant part of the country's, ah, very limited strategic reserve is, ah, in a facility actually owned by a foreign corporation?

**[08:41:42.120] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008862)  
Well, as of now, ah, we have no choice. That's why ang recommendation po ng DOE is magtayo po ng sarili.

**[08:41:48.640] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008863)  
Okay.

**[08:41:49.120] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008864)  
Because, ah, we are also paying rent.

**[08:41:51.360] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008865)  
And is it not also true that the, ah, that facility...

**[08:41:54.880] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008866)  
The Philippine Coastal Storage Facility in Stubic, ah, also, ah, holds, ah, the Navy, ah, the fuel reserves of the U.S. Navy?

**[08:42:08.780] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008867)  
No? Ah, kaya po pagka dumadaong dyan yung mga barko ng U.S. Navy, ah, they refuel from there in the tanks of the Philippine Coastal Storage.

**[08:42:21.400] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008868)  
Hindi po ba ganun din?

**[08:42:23.180] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008869)  
Ang issue po dito is...

**[08:42:24.880] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008870)  
Hindi po ba magiging vulnerable ang ating significant, ah, although meager, strategic reserves, ah, since they are stored, ah, collocated, no, in the same facilities as U.S. Navy facilities?

**[08:42:44.360] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008871)  
Alam po natin yung nangyari. Pinakita na ng gera sa Iran. Target po yan. No, ganun na nangyari. Tinarget yung mga fuel depo sa Bahrain.

**[08:42:54.880] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008872)  
Sa Kuwait, sa UAE, at iba pa. Hindi po ba pinag-iisipan to ng Department of Energy? In other words, security. The security of our energy reserves. Paano po yun?

**[08:43:08.680] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008873)  
Actually, ah, Mr. Speaker, um, yung information po, number one, di po alam ng DOE. Ah, thank you for informing us regarding yung sa military, et cetera.

**[08:43:20.880] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008874)  
Okay. Ah, po.

**[08:43:21.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008875)  
But, um, hindi po natin hiningi yung gera ng...

**[08:43:24.720] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008876)  
American Iran. And, ah, naghanap po tayo ng secured na pening paglagyan ng reserve po natin. And dun po natin nakita po sa Subic. That's why we did it. So, because we have no choice. Wala po tayong sariling depo. That's why, ah, we were forced to good na gamitin po yun. Pwede mas malaki po ang problema ng ekonomiya pag napabayaan po natin at wala po tayong diesel sa ating bansa.

**[08:43:54.720] Rep. Antonio L. Tinio** (asr-008877)

**[08:48:21.980] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008920)

**[08:48:22.900] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008921)

**[08:48:25.400] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008922)

CEDC completed September 15, PEDC, October 3, GNPK, September 20, TSI Unit 1, September 19, TSI Unit 2, September 8.

**[08:48:43.440] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008924)

**[08:48:53.080] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008925)  
2026

**[08:48:53.640] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008926)  
To buffer supply gaps.

**[08:48:56.340] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008927)

**[08:49:09.700] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008928)

We also

**[08:49:32.080] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-008931)

**[09:07:25.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009244)  
for example,

**[09:07:26.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009245)  
solar,

**[09:07:27.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009246)  
bababa na nga.

**[09:07:28.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009247)  
Pero,

**[09:07:29.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009248)  
dalawa na yung binayaran mo.

**[09:07:31.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009249)  
Upgrading sa transmission

**[09:07:33.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009250)  
at saka yung cost ng solar.

**[09:07:36.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009251)  
Hindi pa magtapos dyan.

**[09:07:38.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009252)  
Dahil intermitensya,

**[09:07:40.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009253)  
mangontrata ka na naman ng

**[09:07:42.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009254)  
aspa.

**[09:07:44.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009255)  
Kaya, tatlo na yung binayaran mo.

**[09:07:46.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009256)  
What I am saying is,

**[09:07:48.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009257)  
we should manage

**[09:07:50.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009258)  
the expectation of the public.

**[09:07:53.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009259)  
Kasi, ang tinitinan lang natin,

**[09:07:55.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009260)  
yung pababa na presyo

**[09:07:57.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009261)  
ni renewable.

**[09:07:59.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009262)  
But, yung mga side effect,

**[09:08:02.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009263)  
pag i-infuse mo siya,

**[09:08:04.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009264)  
mayroon ka pang gagawin

**[09:08:06.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009265)  
doon sa other parts of the supply chain.

**[09:08:10.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009266)  
Kaya, hindi makukuha,

**[09:08:12.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009267)  
mag-alboroto yung mga tao.

**[09:08:13.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009268)  
Sabi niyo,

**[09:08:14.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009269)  
pag mag-renewable tayo,

**[09:08:16.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009270)  
I agree kung mga rooftop yan.

**[09:08:19.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009271)  
Kasi, hindi naman natin

**[09:08:22.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009272)  
i-upgrade yung system,

**[09:08:23.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009273)  
dahil sarili mo yan.

**[09:08:25.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009274)  
Kaya, binabalangkas na natin na mabilisan

**[09:08:28.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009275)  
yung sariling kurinti.

**[09:08:30.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009276)  
Rooftop yan.

**[09:08:31.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009277)  
No?

**[09:08:32.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009278)  
So, kindly revisit that policy.

**[09:08:36.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009279)  
Number 3.

**[09:08:37.080] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-009280)  
Yes, we will revisit the policy.

**[09:08:39.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009281)  
Number 3.

**[09:08:40.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009282)  
Yung aspa natin,

**[09:08:44.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009283)  
tinitingnan ko yung datos from the NGCP,

**[09:08:47.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009284)  
pababa ng pababa,

**[09:08:49.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009285)  
yung binabayaran natin sa aspa.

**[09:08:52.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009286)  
Pero pataas ng pataas

**[09:08:54.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009287)  
yung binabayaran natin sa reserve market.

**[09:08:58.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009288)  
Mayroong mga planta ngayon

**[09:09:00.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009289)  
na talagang sinasadya nilang

**[09:09:05.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009290)  
i-breach yung contract

**[09:09:07.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009291)  
with NGCP

**[09:09:09.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009292)  
para materminate.

**[09:09:11.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009293)  
At kung materminate na to,

**[09:09:12.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009294)  
yung capacity na na-pre-up

**[09:09:14.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009295)  
sa market nila ibabato.

**[09:09:17.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009296)  
So, I hope nakita to ng Department of Energy,

**[09:09:21.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009297)  
at saka IRC,

**[09:09:23.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009298)  
may datos naman tayo,

**[09:09:25.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009299)  
pinapaikutan ng mga maroronong

**[09:09:28.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009300)  
yung weaknesses ng ating mga policy.

**[09:09:33.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009301)  
Anong nangyari?

**[09:09:35.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009302)  
Kung marami akong planta,

**[09:09:38.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009303)  
Lawson, Visayas, Mindanao,

**[09:09:40.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009304)  
pwede kong patayin.

**[09:09:42.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009305)  
I'm not saying na sinadya nila,

**[09:09:44.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009306)  
but, kasi mahirap i-prove yun,

**[09:09:47.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009307)  
but nakikita ko yung trend doon sa dito.

**[09:09:50.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009308)  
Pwede kong i-force outage

**[09:09:55.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009309)  
itong planta ko sa Lawson,

**[09:09:57.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009310)  
pero umaandar yung planta ko sa Visayas at Mindanao,

**[09:10:01.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009311)  
or anywhere sa tatlong grid,

**[09:10:04.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009312)  
kikitain ko pa rin

**[09:10:06.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009313)  
yung supposed to be kita ko

**[09:10:10.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009314)  
kung umaandar tong planta na to.

**[09:10:13.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009315)  
Nakatipid pa ko doon sa operating cost,

**[09:10:17.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009316)  
kasi hindi yun umaandar.

**[09:10:19.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009317)  
Kasi yung planta ko na umaandar,

**[09:10:21.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009318)  
doon ko binabato sa reserve market

**[09:10:24.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009319)  
na wala nang katapusan yung pagtaas,

**[09:10:27.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009320)  
except kung magkaroon ng intervention ang ERC.

**[09:10:31.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009321)  
Wira sa ASPA, i-be-beading mo yun,

**[09:10:34.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009322)  
i-CCSP mo yun.

**[09:10:36.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009323)  
At kahit ano mangyari,

**[09:10:38.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009324)  
naka ano yung threshold mo doon sa bid price,

**[09:10:43.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009325)  
pero sa reserve market, wala.

**[09:10:46.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009326)  
Recommendation,

**[09:10:48.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009327)  
kindly revisit the circular on reserve market.

**[09:10:53.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009328)  
Baka pupwede wala nang reserve market.

**[09:10:56.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009329)  
Anyway, i-allow na lang natin na kung kukulangin,

**[09:11:00.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009330)  
bibili na lang si NGCP sa regular market,

**[09:11:05.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009331)  
instead na magkaroon pa talaga tayo ng specific na reserve market.

**[09:11:11.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009332)  
Titignan nyo yung data. May data ako.

**[09:11:14.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009333)  
Billionist na, billionist yung binabayaran,

**[09:11:17.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009334)  
yung transaction sa reserve market,

**[09:11:20.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009335)  
in billions.

**[09:11:21.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009336)  
Pero yung sa ASPA, million lang.

**[09:11:24.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009337)  
When in fact, dapat baligtad yun,

**[09:11:27.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009338)  
si ASPA ang billionist dahil sa mga forced outages,

**[09:11:31.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009339)  
dapat si reserve market ang maliit.

**[09:11:35.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009340)  
Pero doon nga nila binato lahat

**[09:11:37.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009341)  
kasi mas malaki ang kita nila.

**[09:11:40.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009342)  
So, again, recommendation,

**[09:11:42.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009343)  
kindly revisit the circular on reserve market.

**[09:11:45.080] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-009344)  
Yes, we will revisit that.

**[09:11:46.080] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-009345)  
Yes, we will revisit the circular of the reserve market

**[09:11:49.080] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-009346)  
and even the ancillary, Mr. Speaker.

**[09:11:52.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009347)  
Now, another recommendation.

**[09:11:57.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009348)  
Maybe ginawa na to ng DOE, pero sasabihin ko pa rin.

**[09:12:03.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009349)  
Yung mga planta sa Visayas,

**[09:12:06.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009350)  
kailangan tayo magkaroon ng inventory.

**[09:12:09.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009351)  
And even in Mindanao, hindi lang yung installed.

**[09:12:14.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009352)  
You make an inventory on the installed capacity

**[09:12:18.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009353)  
and yung dependable capacity.

**[09:12:21.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009354)  
Kasi magkaiba po yung dalawa.

**[09:12:23.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009355)  
Alam nating lahat yun.

**[09:12:25.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009356)  
Nag-installed ka ng 100 mega,

**[09:12:27.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009357)  
pero ano yung dependable?

**[09:12:29.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009358)  
Baka 20 na lang.

**[09:12:30.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009359)  
Kasi luma na ng mga planta.

**[09:12:33.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009360)  
Kaya mali-mali yung projection na ginagamit natin.

**[09:12:37.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009361)  
So, Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Sponsor,

**[09:12:42.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009362)  
hindi na kailangan sagutin.

**[09:12:43.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009363)  
Kasi sabi ko, baka nga ginawa na ng DOE yun.

**[09:12:48.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009364)  
Kasi ang kasunod diyan,

**[09:12:50.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009365)  
inventory ng ating transmission facilities.

**[09:12:55.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009366)  
Saan ba sa ating transmission facilities

**[09:13:01.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009367)  
ang kaya pang mag-absorb ng additional capacity

**[09:13:07.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009368)  
na hindi na kailangan i-upgrade?

**[09:13:10.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009369)  
This is in connection,

**[09:13:12.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009370)  
doon sa renewable na i-infuse natin,

**[09:13:14.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009371)  
na gusto nating i-dagdag.

**[09:13:17.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009372)  
Kasi kung, to avoid,

**[09:13:19.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009373)  
to avoid yung upgrading ng transmission line,

**[09:13:25.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009374)  
pag-inject ng renewable,

**[09:13:27.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009375)  
dapat may inventory na tayo.

**[09:13:30.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009376)  
Aling banda, for example, sa Visayas,

**[09:13:33.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009377)  
nasa Iloilo ba?

**[09:13:35.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009378)  
O nasa Negros ba?

**[09:13:37.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009379)  
Yung upgraded na nalinyan ng NGCP,

**[09:13:40.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009380)  
na maliit lang yun.

**[09:13:42.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009381)  
Doon natin ituro ang mga,

**[09:13:47.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009382)  
ang mga developer,

**[09:13:50.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009383)  
mga supplier ng renewable.

**[09:13:53.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009384)  
Doon kayo mag-put up,

**[09:13:55.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009385)  
kasi maluwag pa yung transmission doon.

**[09:14:00.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009386)  
Pwede pa niyang i-absorb yung capacity ninyo

**[09:14:03.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009387)  
na wala tayong gasto sa upgrading ng linya.

**[09:14:07.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009388)  
Ginawa na ba ng DOE yan?

**[09:14:13.080] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-009389)  
Yes, Mr. Speaker.

**[09:14:15.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009390)  
Sigurado kayo?

**[09:14:18.080] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-009391)  
Yes.

**[09:14:20.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009392)  
Alam ko na hindi ninyo masagot yan.

**[09:14:28.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009393)  
Kasi sa ngayon, pinabayaan ninyo kung saan saan.

**[09:14:33.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009394)  
Sila ang, sila lang ang nagdesisyon.

**[09:14:36.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009395)  
Kung saan nila ilagay.

**[09:14:38.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009396)  
Resulta, kung malayo sa transmission yan,

**[09:14:42.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009397)  
gagastos na naman tayo ng Thailand.

**[09:14:45.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009398)  
Gagawa na naman tayo ng linya,

**[09:14:47.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009399)  
madidili na naman sila,

**[09:14:49.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009400)  
kung madili yung approval ng ARC,

**[09:14:52.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009401)  
kung may linya na,

**[09:14:54.080] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009402)  
magbabayad na naman tayo sa transmission, Sir.

**[09:14:57.080] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-009403)  
We're doing that already, Mr. Speaker.

**[09:15:00.000] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009404)  
I hope na hindi tayo nagsasayang ng ano dito kasi kanina talagang sinadya kong magpahuli because I'm taking notes of the discussion, dinirekta ko na policy recommendation.

**[09:15:17.760] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009405)  
Marami tayong magagawa, mapababa natin yung presyo without the need of legislation.

**[09:15:24.160] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009406)  
But we must admit na kailangan natin i-legislate.

**[09:15:27.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009407)  
For example, sa ancillary, we are always at the mercy of the private sector dahil sa section 47 na yan.

**[09:15:38.100] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009408)  
So, palaging tinuturo ng ating mamamayan ay palpak na naman yung DOE na yan dahil it is beyond your control.

**[09:15:50.340] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009409)  
Nasa portfolio na ganito pero hindi nagtayo.

**[09:15:54.320] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009410)  
So, ang dapat sa ancillary,

**[09:15:57.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009411)  
within our control yung planta.

**[09:16:00.420] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009412)  
The only way to do that, kahit initially, Luzon, Visayas, Mindanao, na main grid, may isang planta man lang ang napukor.

**[09:16:10.540] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009413)  
So, para may pabalik yung napukor sa generation, ancillary, hindi naman libre yun, babayaran naman ang gobyerno niyan.

**[09:16:19.640] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009414)  
We must amend section 47 of IPIRA.

**[09:16:23.720] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009415)  
Kindly support that amendment, DOE.

**[09:16:27.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009416)  
Kaya, mapabilis, one-time subsidy ng gobyerno, other countries, highly subsidized, tayo, true cost.

**[09:16:36.260] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009417)  
Kahit ibalik lang natin one-time subsidy pag tayo ng planta, operation and maintenance, as pa na.

**[09:16:43.160] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009418)  
Isang business lang tayo gagastos.

**[09:16:45.040] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-009419)  
I totally agree with Congressman Sergio Dago.

**[09:16:48.260] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-009420)  
If I can recommend also, since last term na po ni Congressman Dago,

**[09:16:52.180] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-009421)  
pagkatapos ng term niya, dapat po pumasok sa DOE.

**[09:16:57.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009422)  
Ito, Mr. Speaker, Distinguished Sponsor.

**[09:17:01.560] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-009423)  
No, it's no joke. I mean, with all due respect, alam na alam po ni Congressman Dago.

**[09:17:08.160] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-009424)  
Nagsimula, lineman, ngayong congressman, now we're talking about policy.

**[09:17:12.780] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-009425)  
So, malaki po ang may tutulong talaga ni Congressman Dago.

**[09:17:15.980] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-009426)  
That's why we are very supportive sa mga suggestions ni Congressman Sergio Dago.

**[09:17:21.980] Rep. Arnie B. Fuentebella** (asr-009427)  
More than the Congressman Presley Dessos.

**[09:17:25.120] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009428)  
Thank you, Distinguished Sponsor.

**[09:17:27.600] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009429)  
Policy recommendation, magkahalo ito on Friday sa DOF-PIR.

**[09:17:35.280] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009430)  
Magkahalo ito.

**[09:18:50.340] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009442)

**[09:18:56.840] Rep. Sergio C. Dagooc** (asr-009444)
